   73: function parseIntegerFlag(
   74:   args,
   75:   name,
   76:   fallback = null
   77: ) {
   78:   const prefix = `--${name}=`;
   79: 
   80:   const match = args.find(
   81:     (value) =>
   82:       String(value).startsWith(prefix)
   83:   );
   84: 
   85:   if (!match) {
   86:     return fallback;

   90:     String(match).slice(prefix.length)

   95:     number < 1
   96:   ) {
   97:     throw new Error(

   99:     );
  100:   }
  101: 
  102:   return number;
  103: }

  248:   map,
  249:   key
  250: ) {
  251:   if (!map[key]) {
  252:     map[key] = {
  253:       evaluated: 0,
  254:       top1Hits: 0,
  255:       top3Hits: 0,
  256:       errors: 0,
  257:     };
  258:   }
  259: 
  260:   return map[key];
  261: }
  262: 
  263: function finalizeBucket(bucket = {}) {
  264:   const evaluated = Number(
  265:     bucket.evaluated || 0
  266:   );
  267: 
  268:   const top1Hits = Number(
  269:     bucket.top1Hits || 0
  270:   );
  271: 
  272:   const top3Hits = Number(
  273:     bucket.top3Hits || 0
  274:   );
  275: 
  276:   return {
  277:     evaluated,
  278:     top1Hits,
  279:     top3Hits,
  280:     errors: Number(
  281:       bucket.errors || 0
  282:     ),
  283:     top1Rate:
  284:       evaluated > 0
  285:         ? Number(
  286:             (
  287:               top1Hits /
  288:               evaluated *
  289:               100
  290:             ).toFixed(4)
  291:           )
  292:         : 0,
  293:     top3Rate:
  294:       evaluated > 0
  295:         ? Number(
  296:             (
  297:               top3Hits /
  298:               evaluated *
  299:               100
  300:             ).toFixed(4)
  301:           )
  302:         : 0,
  303:   };
  304: }
  305: 
  306: function finalizeMap(map = {}) {
  307:   const result = {};
  308: 
  309:   for (
  310:     const [key, bucket]
  311:     of Object.entries(map)
  312:   ) {
  313:     result[key] = finalizeBucket(
  314:       bucket
  315:     );
  316:   }
  317: 
  318:   return result;
  319: }
  320: 
  321: function formatPercent(value) {
  322:   return `${Number(value || 0).toFixed(2)}%`;
  323: }
  324: 
  325: function buildTextReport(result) {
  326:   const lines = [];
  327: 
  328:   lines.push(
  329:     "=============================================="
  330:   );
  331: 
  332:   lines.push(
  333:     "TOP3 OFFICIAL BACKTEST"
  334:   );
  335: 
  336:   lines.push(
  337:     "=============================================="
  338:   );
  339: 
  340:   lines.push("");
  341: 
  342:   lines.push(
  343:     `Loteria.............: ${result.lotteryKey}`
  344:   );
  345: 
  346:   lines.push(
  347:     `Engine..............: ${result.engine}`
  348:   );
  349: 
  350:   lines.push(
  351:     `Histórico carregado.: ${result.historyLoaded}`
  352:   );
  353: 
  354:   lines.push(
  355:     `Casos elegíveis.....: ${result.eligibleCases}`
  356:   );
  357: 
  358:   lines.push(
  359:     `Casos avaliados.....: ${result.global.evaluated}`
  360:   );
  361: 
  362:   lines.push(
  363:     `Casos ignorados.....: ${result.skipped}`
  364:   );
  365: 
  366:   lines.push(
  367:     `Erros...............: ${result.global.errors}`
  368:   );
  369: 
  370:   lines.push(
  371:     `Período histórico...: ${result.historyPeriod.from} até ${result.historyPeriod.to}`
  372:   );
  373: 
  374:   lines.push(
  375:     `Período avaliado....: ${result.evaluationPeriod.from || "-"} até ${result.evaluationPeriod.to || "-"}`
  376:   );
  377: 
  378:   lines.push(
  379:     `TOP1................: ${result.global.top1Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.top1Rate)})`
  380:   );
  381: 
  382:   lines.push(
  383:     `TOP3................: ${result.global.top3Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.top3Rate)})`
  384:   );
  385: 
  386:   lines.push(
  387:     `Tempo total.........: ${result.tookMs} ms`
  388:   );
  389: 
  390:   lines.push("");
  391:   lines.push("POR HORÁRIO");
  392: 
  393:   for (
  394:     const [hour, bucket]
  395:     of Object.entries(result.byHour)
  396:   ) {
  397:     lines.push(
  398:       `${hour.padEnd(8)} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | TOP3=${formatPercent(bucket.top3Rate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
  399:     );
  400:   }
  401: 
  402:   lines.push("");
  403:   lines.push("POR DIA DA SEMANA");
  404: 
  405:   for (
  406:     const [weekday, bucket]
  407:     of Object.entries(result.byWeekday)
  408:   ) {
  409:     lines.push(
  410:       `${weekday.padEnd(3)} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | TOP3=${formatPercent(bucket.top3Rate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
  411:     );
  412:   }
  413: 
  414:   lines.push("");
  415:   lines.push("POR MÊS");
  416: 
  417:   for (
  418:     const [month, bucket]
  419:     of Object.entries(result.byMonth)
  420:   ) {
  421:     lines.push(
  422:       `${month} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | TOP3=${formatPercent(bucket.top3Rate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
  423:     );
  424:   }
  425: 
  426:   return lines.join("\n");
  427: }
  428: 
  429: async function runOfficialBacktest(
  430:   options = {},
  431:   dependencies = {}
  432: ) {
  433:   const startedAt = Date.now();
  434: 
  435:   const lotteryKey =
  436:     normalizeLotteryKey(
  437:       options.lotteryKey ||
  438:       "PT_RIO"
  439:     );
  440: 
  441:   const publicApi =
  442:     dependencies.publicApi ||
  443:     loadTop3PublicApi();
  444: 
  445:   const loadHistory =
  446:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
  447:     readFullHistory;
  448: 
  449:   const loadMetadata =
  450:     dependencies.readMetadata ||
  451:     readMetadata;
  452: 
  453:   const computeTop3 =
  454:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
  455:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
  456: 
  457:   const history = sortDraws(
  458:     await loadHistory(
  459:       lotteryKey,
  460:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
  461:     ),
  462:     publicApi
  463:   );
  464: 
  465:   if (!history.length) {
  466:     throw new Error(
  467:       "Histórico TOP3 vazio."
  468:     );
  469:   }
  470: 
  471:   const metadataResult =
  472:     await loadMetadata(
  473:       lotteryKey,
  474:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
  475:     );
  476: 
  477:   const metadata =
  478:     metadataResult?.data || null;
  479: 
  480:   if (
  481:     dependencies.allowIncompleteSnapshot !== true &&
  482:     (
  483:       metadataResult?.exists !== true ||
  484:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus !==
  485:         "complete"
  486:     )
  487:   ) {
  488:     throw new Error(
  489:       "Histórico TOP3 não está marcado como completo."
  490:     );
  491:   }
  492: 
  493:   const minHistory = Number(
  494:     options.minHistory || 100
  495:   );
  496: 
  497:   const from =
  498:     options.from || null;
  499: 
  500:   const to =
  501:     options.to || null;
  502: 
  503:   const eligibleIndexes = [];
  504: 
  505:   for (
  506:     let index = minHistory;
  507:     index < history.length;
  508:     index += 1
  509:   ) {
  510:     const draw = history[index];
  511: 
  512:     const ymd =
  513:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
  514: 
  515:     const hour =
  516:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
  517: 
  518:     if (!ymd || !hour) {
  519:       continue;
  520:     }
  521: 
  522:     if (from && ymd < from) {
  523:       continue;
  524:     }
  525: 
  526:     if (to && ymd > to) {
  527:       continue;
  528:     }
  529: 
  530:     if (
  531:       getFirstPrizeGroup(
  532:         draw,
  533:         publicApi
  534:       ) == null
  535:     ) {
  536:       continue;
  537:     }
  538: 
  539:     eligibleIndexes.push(index);
  540:   }
  541: 
  542:   const selectedIndexes =
  543:     options.limit
  544:       ? eligibleIndexes.slice(
  545:           0,
  546:           Number(options.limit)
  547:         )
  548:       : eligibleIndexes;
  549: 
  550:   const globalBucket = {
  551:     evaluated: 0,
  552:     top1Hits: 0,
  553:     top3Hits: 0,
  554:     errors: 0,
  555:   };
  556: 
  557:   const byHour = {};
  558:   const byWeekday = {};
  559:   const byMonth = {};
  560: 
  561:   let skipped = 0;
  562:   let evaluationFrom = null;
  563:   let evaluationTo = null;
  564: 
  565:   for (
  566:     let position = 0;
  567:     position < selectedIndexes.length;
  568:     position += 1
  569:   ) {
  570:     const index =
  571:       selectedIndexes[position];
  572: 
  573:     const targetDraw =
  574:       history[index];
  575: 
  576:     const targetYmd =
  577:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(
  578:         targetDraw
  579:       );
  580: 
  581:     const targetHour =
  582:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(
  583:         targetDraw
  584:       );
  585: 
  586:     const actualGroup =
  587:       getFirstPrizeGroup(
  588:         targetDraw,
  589:         publicApi
  590:       );
  591: 
  592:     const historyBefore =
  593:       history.slice(0, index);
  594: 
  595:     const drawLast =
  596:       historyBefore[
  597:         historyBefore.length - 1
  598:       ];
  599: 
  600:     if (
  601:       !targetYmd ||
  602:       !targetHour ||
  603:       actualGroup == null ||
  604:       !drawLast
  605:     ) {
  606:       skipped += 1;
  607:       continue;
  608:     }
  609: 
  610:     const weekday = String(
  611:       new Date(
  612:         `${targetYmd}T12:00:00Z`
  613:       ).getUTCDay()
  614:     );
  615: 
  616:     const month =
  617:       targetYmd.slice(0, 7);
  618: 
  619:     const hourBucket =
  620:       ensureBucket(
  621:         byHour,
  622:         normalizeHour(targetHour) ||
  623:         targetHour
  624:       );
  625: 
  626:     const weekdayBucket =
  627:       ensureBucket(
  628:         byWeekday,
  629:         weekday
  630:       );
  631: 
  632:     const monthBucket =
  633:       ensureBucket(
  634:         byMonth,
  635:         month
  636:       );
  637: 
  638:     try {
  639:       const computed =
  640:         computeTop3({
  641:           lotteryKey,
  642:           drawsRange:
  643:             historyBefore,
  644:           drawLast,
  645:           PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  646:           PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  647:           FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  648:           topN: 3,
  649:           targetYmdOverride:
  650:             targetYmd,
  651:           targetHourOverride:
  652:             targetHour,
  653:         });
  654: 
  655:       const predictionGroups =
  656:         getPredictionGroups(
  657:           computed
  658:         );
  659: 
  660:       if (
  661:         predictionGroups.length < 3
  662:       ) {
  663:         throw new Error(
  664:           "Motor retornou menos de 3 grupos."
  665:         );
  666:       }
  667: 
  668:       const top1Hit =
  669:         predictionGroups[0] ===
  670:         actualGroup;
  671: 
  672:       const top3Hit =
  673:         predictionGroups.includes(
  674:           actualGroup
  675:         );
  676: 
  677:       for (const bucket of [
  678:         globalBucket,
  679:         hourBucket,
  680:         weekdayBucket,
  681:         monthBucket,
  682:       ]) {
  683:         bucket.evaluated += 1;
  684: 
  685:         if (top1Hit) {
  686:           bucket.top1Hits += 1;
  687:         }
  688: 
  689:         if (top3Hit) {
  690:           bucket.top3Hits += 1;
  691:         }
  692:       }
  693: 
  694:       evaluationFrom =
  695:         evaluationFrom || targetYmd;
  696: 
  697:       evaluationTo = targetYmd;
  698:     } catch (error) {
  699:       globalBucket.errors += 1;
  700:       hourBucket.errors += 1;
  701:       weekdayBucket.errors += 1;
  702:       monthBucket.errors += 1;
  703: 
  704:       if (
  705:         dependencies.onCaseError
  706:       ) {
  707:         dependencies.onCaseError({
  708:           index,
  709:           targetYmd,
  710:           targetHour,
  711:           error,
  712:         });
  713:       }
  714:     }
  715: 
  716:     const done = position + 1;
  717: 
  718:     if (
  719:       options.progress !== false &&
  720:       (
  721:         done % 25 === 0 ||
  722:         done === selectedIndexes.length
  723:       )
  724:     ) {
  725:       console.log(
  726:         `[BACKTEST] ${done}/${selectedIndexes.length} | avaliados=${globalBucket.evaluated} | erros=${globalBucket.errors}`
  727:       );
  728:     }
  729:   }
  730: 
  731:   const result = {
  732:     ok: true,
  733:     lotteryKey,
  734:     engine:
  735:       "top3_statistical_v3",
  736:     historySource:
  737:       "snapshot",
  738:     historyLoaded:
  739:       history.length,
  740:     eligibleCases:
  741:       eligibleIndexes.length,
  742:     selectedCases:
  743:       selectedIndexes.length,
  744:     skipped,
  745:     minHistory,
  746:     limit:
  747:       options.limit || null,
  748:     historyPeriod: {
  749:       from:
  750:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(
  751:           history[0]
  752:         ) || null,
  753:       to:
  754:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(
  755:           history[
  756:             history.length - 1
  757:           ]
  758:         ) || null,
  759:     },
  760:     evaluationPeriod: {
  761:       from: evaluationFrom,
  762:       to: evaluationTo,
  763:     },
  764:     global:
  765:       finalizeBucket(
  766:         globalBucket
  767:       ),
  768:     byHour:
  769:       finalizeMap(byHour),
  770:     byWeekday:
  771:       finalizeMap(byWeekday),
  772:     byMonth:
  773:       finalizeMap(byMonth),
  774:     metadata: {
  775:       bootstrapStatus:
  776:         metadata?.bootstrapStatus ||
  777:         null,
  778:       totalStored:
  779:         Number(
  780:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0
  781:         ) || null,
  782:       lastProcessedDrawId:
  783:         metadata?.lastProcessedDrawId ||
  784:         null,
  785:     },
  786:     tookMs:
  787:       Date.now() - startedAt,
  788:   };
  789: 
  790:   return result;
  791: }
  792: 
  793: async function main() {
  794:   const options = parseCliArgs(
  795:     process.argv.slice(2)
  796:   );
  797: 
  798:   console.log("");
  799:   console.log(
  800:     "===================================="
  801:   );
  802:   console.log(
  803:     "TOP3 OFFICIAL BACKTEST"
  804:   );
  805:   console.log(
  806:     "===================================="
  807:   );
  808:   console.log("");
  809:   console.log(
  810:     "Loteria............:",
  811:     options.lotteryKey
  812:   );
  813:   console.log(
  814:     "Limite.............:",
  815:     options.limit || "TODOS"
  816:   );
  817:   console.log(
  818:     "Histórico mínimo...:",
  819:     options.minHistory
  820:   );
  821:   console.log(
  822:     "Período............:",
  823:     `${options.from || "início"} até ${options.to || "fim"}`
  824:   );
  825:   console.log("");
  826: 
  827:   const result =
  828:     await runOfficialBacktest(
  829:       options
  830:     );
  831: 
  832:   const outputDir = path.resolve(
  833:     options.outputDir
  834:   );
  835: 
  836:   fs.mkdirSync(
  837:     outputDir,
  838:     {
  839:       recursive: true,
  840:     }
  841:   );
  842: 
  843:   const suffix =
  844:     options.limit
  845:       ? `limit_${options.limit}`
  846:       : "full";
  847: 
  848:   const jsonPath = path.join(
  849:     outputDir,
  850:     `top3_official_backtest_${suffix}.json`
  851:   );
  852: 
  853:   const txtPath = path.join(
  854:     outputDir,
  855:     `top3_official_backtest_${suffix}.txt`
  856:   );
  857: 
  858:   fs.writeFileSync(
  859:     jsonPath,
  860:     JSON.stringify(
  861:       result,
  862:       null,
  863:       2
  864:     ),
  865:     "utf8"
  866:   );
  867: 
  868:   fs.writeFileSync(
  869:     txtPath,
  870:     buildTextReport(result),
  871:     "utf8"
  872:   );
  873: 
  874:   console.log("");
  875:   console.log(
  876:     buildTextReport(result)
  877:   );
  878:   console.log("");
  879:   console.log(
  880:     "JSON:",
  881:     jsonPath
  882:   );
  883:   console.log(
  884:     "TXT :",
  885:     txtPath
  886:   );
  887: }
  888: 
  889: if (require.main === module) {
  890:   main().catch((error) => {
  891:     console.error(
  892:       error?.stack ||
  893:       error?.message ||
  894:       error
  895:     );
  896: 
  897:     process.exit(1);
  898:   });
  899: }
  900: 
  901: module.exports = {
  902:   parseCliArgs,
  903:   sortDraws,
  904:   getFirstPrizeGroup,
  905:   getPredictionGroups,
  906:   finalizeBucket,
  907:   buildTextReport,
  908:   runOfficialBacktest,
  909: };

backend/engine/top3PredictionService.js

    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const {
    4:   fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange,
    5: } = require("./drawRepository");
    6: 
    7: const {
    8:   readFullHistory,
    9:   readMetadata,
   10: } = require("./top3HistoryRepository");
   11: 
   12: const {
   13:   computeStatisticalTop3V3,
   14:   loadTop3PublicApi,
   15: } = require("./scoreEngineUnified");
   16: 
   17: const {
   18:   createPredictionRun,
   19: } = require("./predictionService");
   20: 
   21: const {
   22:   getDb,
   23: } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
   24: 
   25: const PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL = [
   26:   "09:00",
   27:   "11:00",
   28:   "14:00",
   29:   "16:00",
   30:   "18:00",
   31:   "21:00",
   32: ];
   33:

   35:   "09:00",
   36:   "11:00",
   37:   "14:00",
   38:   "16:00",
   39:   "21:00",
   40: ];
   41: 
   42: const FEDERAL_SCHEDULE = [
   43:   "20:00",
   44: ];
   45: 
   46: const ANIMALS = {
   47:   "01": "Avestruz",
   48:   "02": "Águia",
   49:   "03": "Burro",

   61:   "15": "Jacaré",

   73: 
   74: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
   75:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
   76:     .trim()
   77:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
   78: }
   79: 
   80: function normalizeYmd(value) {
   81:   const text = String(value || "").trim();
   82: 
   83:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text)) {
   84:     throw new Error(
   85:       "Data inválida. Utilize o formato YYYY-MM-DD."
   86:     );
   87:   }
   88: 
   89:   return text;
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function normalizeHour(value) {
   93:   const text = String(value || "")
   94:     .trim()
   95:     .replace(/[hH]/g, ":");
   96: 
   97:   const match = text.match(
   98:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/
   99:   );
  100: 
  101:   if (!match) {
  102:     throw new Error(
  103:       "Horário inválido. Utilize HH:MM."
  104:     );
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
  108:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
  109: 
  110:   if (
  111:     hour < 0 ||
  112:     hour > 23 ||
  113:     minute < 0 ||
  114:     minute > 59
  115:   ) {
  116:     throw new Error("Horário fora do intervalo válido.");
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
  120:     minute
  121:   ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  122: }
  123: 
  124: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
  125:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour)}`;
  126: }
  127: 
  128: function subtractDaysYmd(ymd, days) {
  129:   const safeDays = Math.max(
  130:     1,
  131:     Math.min(1460, Number(days || 180))
  132:   );
  133: 
  134:   const date = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00Z`);
  135: 
  136:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
  137:     throw new Error(
  138:       "Não foi possível calcular a janela histórica."
  139:     );
  140:   }
  141: 
  142:   date.setUTCDate(
  143:     date.getUTCDate() - safeDays
  144:   );
  145: 
  146:   return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  147: }
  148: 
  149: function normalizeHistorySource(value) {
  150:   const source = String(value || "auto")
  151:     .trim()
  152:     .toLowerCase();
  153: 
  154:   if (
  155:     source !== "auto" &&
  156:     source !== "snapshot" &&
  157:     source !== "range"
  158:   ) {
  159:     throw new Error(
  160:       "historySource inválido. Utilize auto, snapshot ou range."
  161:     );
  162:   }
  163: 
  164:   return source;
  165: }
  166: 
  167: async function loadPredictionHistory({
  168:   lotteryKey,
  169:   date,
  170:   input = {},
  171:   dependencies = {},
  172: } = {}) {
  173:   const requestedSource = normalizeHistorySource(
  174:     input.historySource
  175:   );
  176: 
  177:   const loadMetadata =
  178:     dependencies.readHistoryMetadata ||
  179:     readMetadata;
  180: 
  181:   const loadFullHistory =
  182:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
  183:     readFullHistory;
  184: 
  185:   const loadRangeHistory =
  186:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
  187:     fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange;
  188: 
  189:   if (
  190:     requestedSource === "auto" ||
  191:     requestedSource === "snapshot"
  192:   ) {
  193:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
  194:       lotteryKey,
  195:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
  196:     );
  197: 
  198:     const metadata =
  199:       metadataResult?.data || null;
  200: 
  201:     const snapshotReady =
  202:       metadataResult?.exists === true &&
  203:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus === "complete";
  204: 
  205:     if (snapshotReady) {
  206:       const draws = await loadFullHistory(
  207:         lotteryKey,
  208:         dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
  209:       );
  210: 
  211:       if (Array.isArray(draws) && draws.length) {
  212:         const expectedTotal = Number(
  213:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0
  214:         );
  215: 
  216:         if (
  217:           expectedTotal > 0 &&
  218:           draws.length !== expectedTotal
  219:         ) {
  220:           throw new Error(
  221:             "Histórico TOP3 inconsistente: metadata.totalDraws=" +
  222:             `${expectedTotal}, carregados=${draws.length}.`
  223:           );
  224:         }
  225: 
  226:         return {
  227:           source: "snapshot",
  228:           draws,
  229:           metadata,
  230:           lookbackDays: null,
  231:           maxDraws: null,
  232:           startYmd:
  233:             metadata?.firstYmd || null,
  234:         };
  235:       }
  236: 
  237:       throw new Error(
  238:         "Metadata do histórico TOP3 está completo, mas nenhum draw foi carregado."
  239:       );
  240:     }
  241: 
  242:     if (requestedSource === "snapshot") {
  243:       throw new Error(
  244:         "Histórico TOP3 completo ainda não está disponível."
  245:       );
  246:     }
  247:   }
  248: 
  249:   const lookbackDays = Math.max(
  250:     30,
  251:     Math.min(
  252:       1460,
  253:       Number(input.lookbackDays || 180)
  254:     )
  255:   );
  256: 
  257:   const maxDraws = Math.max(
  258:     100,
  259:     Math.min(
  260:       5000,
  261:       Number(input.maxDraws || 1200)
  262:     )
  263:   );
  264: 
  265:   const startYmd = subtractDaysYmd(
  266:     date,
  267:     lookbackDays
  268:   );
  269: 
  270:   const rawDraws = await loadRangeHistory({
  271:     lottery: lotteryKey,
  272:     startYmd,
  273:     endYmd: date,
  274:     pageSize: Number(input.pageSize || 250),
  275:     maxDraws,
  276:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
  277:       input.prizeConcurrency || 24
  278:     ),
  279:   });
  280: 
  281:   return {
  282:     source: "range_fallback",
  283:     draws: extractDraws(rawDraws),
  284:     metadata: null,
  285:     lookbackDays,
  286:     maxDraws,
  287:     startYmd,
  288:   };
  289: }
  290: 
  291: function extractDraws(result) {
  292:   if (Array.isArray(result)) {
  293:     return result;
  294:   }
  295: 
  296:   const candidates = [
  297:     result?.draws,
  298:     result?.items,
  299:     result?.results,
  300:     result?.data,
  301:   ];
  302: 
  303:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
  304:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
  305:       return candidate;
  306:     }
  307:   }
  308: 
  309:   return [];
  310: }
  311: 
  312: function normalizePublicMilhar(value) {
  313:   const digits = String(value || "")
  314:     .replace(/\D+/g, "");
  315: 
  316:   if (!digits) {
  317:     return "";
  318:   }
  319: 
  320:   return digits
  321:     .slice(-4)
  322:     .padStart(4, "0");
  323: }
  324: 
  325: function publicHourCode(value) {
  326:   return normalizeHour(value)
  327:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
  328:     .slice(0, 2)
  329:     .padStart(2, "0");
  330: }
  331: 
  332: function resolveNextTop3Slot({
  333:   lotteryKey,
  334:   ymd,
  335:   hour,
  336:   publicApi,
  337: }) {
  338:   if (
  339:     !publicApi ||
  340:     typeof publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery !== "function"
  341:   ) {
  342:     throw new Error(
  343:       "API pública TOP3 sem getNextSlotForLottery."
  344:     );
  345:   }
  346: 
  347:   const resolved =
  348:     publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery({
  349:       lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey),
  350:       ymd: normalizeYmd(ymd),
  351:       hourBucket: normalizeHour(hour),
  352:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  353:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  354:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  355:     });
  356: 
  357:   const rawHour = String(
  358:     resolved?.hour ||
  359:     resolved?.hourBucket ||
  360:     ""
  361:   ).trim();
  362: 
  363:   const hourMatch =
  364:     rawHour.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::00|h)?$/i);
  365: 
  366:   const canonicalHour = hourMatch
  367:     ? `${String(Number(hourMatch[1])).padStart(2, "0")}:00`
  368:     : "";
  369: 
  370:   return {
  371:     ...resolved,
  372:     ymd: normalizeYmd(resolved?.ymd),
  373:     hour: canonicalHour,
  374:     hourBucket: canonicalHour,
  375:   };
  376: }
  377: 
  378: function scheduleForPublicProjection(
  379:   lotteryKey,
  380:   date
  381: ) {
  382:   if (normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey) === "FEDERAL") {
  383:     return [...FEDERAL_SCHEDULE];
  384:   }
  385: 
  386:   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
  387:   const dow = parsed.getUTCDay();
  388: 
  389:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) {
  390:     return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT];
  391:   }
  392: 
  393:   return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL];
  394: }
  395: 
  396: function buildPublicMilharesCols(
  397:   engineOutput,
  398:   expectedCols = 4,
  399:   perCol = 5
  400: ) {
  401:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.dezenas)
  402:     ? engineOutput.dezenas
  403:     : [];
  404: 
  405:   const slots = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.slots)
  406:     ? engineOutput.slots
  407:     : [];
  408: 
  409:   const cols = [];
  410: 
  411:   for (const dezena of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
  412:     const items = slots
  413:       .filter(
  414:         (slot) =>
  415:           String(slot?.dezena || "") ===
  416:           String(dezena || "")
  417:       )
  418:       .map(
  419:         (slot) =>
  420:           normalizePublicMilhar(slot?.milhar)
  421:       )
  422:       .filter(
  423:         (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
  424:       )
  425:       .slice(0, perCol);
  426: 
  427:     while (items.length < perCol) {
  428:       items.push("");
  429:     }
  430: 
  431:     cols.push({
  432:       dezena: String(dezena || ""),
  433:       items,
  434:     });
  435:   }
  436: 
  437:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
  438:     cols.push({
  439:       dezena: "",
  440:       items: Array(perCol).fill(""),
  441:     });
  442:   }
  443: 
  444:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
  445: }
  446: 
  447: function buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
  448:   computedTop,
  449:   history,
  450:   lotteryKey,
  451:   date,
  452:   closeHour,
  453:   publicApi,
  454: }) {
  455:   const schedule = scheduleForPublicProjection(
  456:     lotteryKey,
  457:     date
  458:   );
  459: 
  460:   return (Array.isArray(computedTop)
  461:     ? computedTop
  462:     : []
  463:   )
  464:     .slice(0, 3)
  465:     .map((item, index) => {
  466:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
  467: 
  468:       if (
  469:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
  470:         grupo < 1 ||
  471:         grupo > 25
  472:       ) {
  473:         return null;
  474:       }
  475: 
  476:       const probability = Number(
  477:         item?.scoreProb ??
  478:         item?.probability ??
  479:         item?.confidence ??
  480:         0
  481:       );
  482: 
  483:       const prob =
  484:         probability > 1
  485:           ? probability / 100
  486:           : probability;
  487: 
  488:       const engineOutput =
  489:         publicApi.build20MilharesForGrupo({
  490:           rangeDraws: history,
  491:           analysisHourBucket: closeHour,
  492:           schedule,
  493:           grupo2: grupo,
  494:           targetYmd: date,
  495:         });
  496: 
  497:       const milharesCols =
  498:         buildPublicMilharesCols(
  499:           engineOutput,
  500:           4,
  501:           5
  502:         );
  503: 
  504:       const milhares20 = milharesCols
  505:         .flatMap((column) => column.items)
  506:         .filter(
  507:           (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
  508:         )
  509:         .slice(0, 20);
  510: 
  511:       return {
  512:         rank: index + 1,
  513:         grupo,
  514:         animal:
  515:           item?.animal ||
  516:           ANIMALS[
  517:             String(grupo).padStart(2, "0")
  518:           ] ||
  519:           "",
  520:         prob:
  521:           Number.isFinite(prob)
  522:             ? prob
  523:             : 0,
  524:         probPct:
  525:           Number.isFinite(prob)
  526:             ? Number((prob * 100).toFixed(4))
  527:             : 0,
  528:         milhares20,
  529:         milharesCols,
  530:         meta: item?.meta || null,
  531:       };
  532:     })
  533:     .filter(Boolean);
  534: }
  535: 
  536: async function saveTop3PublicProjection({
  537:   lotteryKey,
  538:   date,
  539:   closeHour,
  540:   snapshot,
  541:   engineVersion = "V3_STATISTICAL",
  542:   source = "backend-top3",
  543: }) {
  544:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
  545:   const hour = normalizeHour(closeHour);
  546:   const hourCode = publicHourCode(hour);
  547: 
  548:   const id =
  549:     `${lottery}__${date}__${hourCode}`;
  550: 
  551:   const normalizedSnapshot =
  552:     Array.isArray(snapshot)
  553:       ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
  554:       : [];
  555: 
  556:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
  557:     throw new Error(
  558:       "Snapshot público TOP3 vazio."
  559:     );
  560:   }
  561: 
  562:   const picks = normalizedSnapshot
  563:     .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
  564:     .filter(
  565:       (grupo) =>
  566:         Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
  567:         grupo >= 1 &&
  568:         grupo <= 25
  569:     )
  570:     .slice(0, 3);
  571: 
  572:   if (!picks.length) {
  573:     throw new Error(
  574:       "Picks públicos TOP3 inválidos."
  575:     );
  576:   }
  577: 
  578:   const database = getDb();
  579:   const ref = database
  580:     .collection("top3_predictions")
  581:     .doc(id);
  582: 
  583:   const now = Date.now();
  584: 
  585:   const payload = {
  586:     id,
  587:     lotteryKey: lottery,
  588:     targetYmd: date,
  589:     targetHour:
  590:       `${hourCode}h`,
  591:     targetKey:
  592:       `${date}_${hourCode}h`,
  593:     picks,
  594:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
  595:     engineVersion,
  596:     status: "predicted",
  597:     resultGrupo: null,
  598:     resultMilhar: "",
  599:     resultAnimal: "",
  600:     hitType: "",
  601:     hitScore: 0,
  602:     hitPosition: -1,
  603:     matchedValue: "",
  604:     createdAt: now,
  605:     updatedAt: now,
  606:     createdBy: source,
  607:     source,
  608:   };
  609: 
  610:   return database.runTransaction(
  611:     async (transaction) => {
  612:       const current =
  613:         await transaction.get(ref);
  614: 
  615:       if (!current.exists) {
  616:         transaction.set(ref, payload);
  617: 
  618:         return {
  619:           ok: true,
  620:           created: true,
  621:           updated: false,
  622:           existing: false,
  623:           protected: false,
  624:           id,
  625:         };
  626:       }
  627: 
  628:       const currentData =
  629:         current.data() || {};
  630: 
  631:       const currentStatus = String(
  632:         currentData.status || ""
  633:       )
  634:         .trim()
  635:         .toLowerCase();
  636: 
  637:       if (currentStatus === "validated") {
  638:         return {
  639:           ok: true,
  640:           created: false,
  641:           updated: false,
  642:           existing: true,
  643:           protected: true,
  644:           reason: "ALREADY_VALIDATED",
  645:           id,
  646:         };
  647:       }
  648: 
  649:       const updatedPayload = {
  650:         ...payload,
  651:         createdAt:
  652:           currentData.createdAt ||
  653:           payload.createdAt,
  654:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
  655:         resultGrupo:
  656:           currentData.resultGrupo ?? null,
  657:         resultMilhar:
  658:           currentData.resultMilhar || "",
  659:         resultAnimal:
  660:           currentData.resultAnimal || "",
  661:         hitType:
  662:           currentData.hitType || "",
  663:         hitScore:
  664:           Number(currentData.hitScore || 0),
  665:         hitPosition:
  666:           Number.isFinite(
  667:             Number(currentData.hitPosition)
  668:           )
  669:             ? Number(currentData.hitPosition)
  670:             : -1,
  671:         matchedValue:
  672:           currentData.matchedValue || "",
  673:       };
  674: 
  675:       transaction.set(
  676:         ref,
  677:         updatedPayload,
  678:         { merge: true }
  679:       );
  680: 
  681:       return {
  682:         ok: true,
  683:         created: false,
  684:         updated: true,
  685:         existing: true,
  686:         protected: false,
  687:         id,
  688:       };
  689:     }
  690:   );
  691: }
  692: 
  693: function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
  694:   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
  695:     .slice(0, 3)
  696:     .map((item, index) => {
  697:       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);
  698:       const grupo = String(grupoNumber).padStart(2, "0");
  699: 
  700:       const probability = Number(
  701:         item?.scoreProb ??
  702:         item?.probability ??
  703:         item?.confidence ??
  704:         0
  705:       );
  706: 
  707:       const percentage =
  708:         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
  709:           ? probability * 100
  710:           : probability;
  711: 
  712:       return {
  713:         type: "grupo",
  714:         grupo,
  715:         animal:
  716:           item?.animal ||
  717:           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
  718:           null,
  719:         score: Number(
  720:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
  721:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
  722:             : 0
  723:         ),
  724:         confidence: Number(
  725:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
  726:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
  727:             : 0
  728:         ),
  729:         reasons: Array.isArray(item?.reasons)
  730:           ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean)
  731:           : [],
  732:         signals: {
  733:           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
  734:           rankPosition: index + 1,
  735:           scoreProb: Number(item?.scoreProb || 0),
  736:           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
  737:           meta: item?.meta || null,
  738:         },
  739:       };
  740:     })
  741:     .filter((item) => /^\d{2}$/.test(item.grupo));
  742: }
  743: 
  744: async function createTop3PredictionRun(
  745:   input = {},
  746:   dependencies = {}
  747: ) {
  748:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
  749:     input.lotteryKey
  750:   );
  751: 
  752:   const date = normalizeYmd(input.date);
  753:   const closeHour = normalizeHour(input.closeHour);
  754: 
  755:   const computeTop3 =
  756:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
  757:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
  758: 
  759:   const persistRun =
  760:     dependencies.persistRun ||
  761:     createPredictionRun;
  762: 
  763:   const publicApi =
  764:     dependencies.publicApi ||
  765:     loadTop3PublicApi();
  766: 
  767:   const historyLoad = await loadPredictionHistory({
  768:     lotteryKey,
  769:     date,
  770:     input,
  771:     dependencies,
  772:   });
  773: 
  774:   const allDraws = extractDraws(
  775:     historyLoad.draws
  776:   );
  777: 
  778:   const {
  779:     source: historySource,
  780:     metadata: historyMetadata,
  781:     lookbackDays,
  782:     maxDraws,
  783:     startYmd,
  784:   } = historyLoad;
  785: 
  786:   if (!allDraws.length) {
  787:     throw new Error(
  788:       `Nenhum resultado encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
  789:     );
  790:   }
  791: 
  792:   const targetKey = dateHourKey(date, closeHour);
  793: 
  794:   const history = allDraws
  795:     .filter((draw) => {
  796:       const ymd = publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
  797:       const hour = publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
  798: 
  799:       if (!ymd || !hour) {
  800:         return false;
  801:       }
  802: 
  803:       return dateHourKey(ymd, hour) < targetKey;
  804:     })
  805:     .sort((a, b) => {
  806:       const aKey = dateHourKey(
  807:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(a),
  808:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(a)
  809:       );
  810: 
  811:       const bKey = dateHourKey(
  812:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(b),
  813:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(b)
  814:       );
  815: 
  816:       return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
  817:     });
  818: 
  819:   if (!history.length) {
  820:     throw new Error(
  821:       "Não existe histórico anterior ao horário solicitado."
  822:     );
  823:   }
  824: 
  825:   const drawLast = history[history.length - 1];
  826: 
  827:   const drawsToday = history.filter(
  828:     (draw) => publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw) === date
  829:   );
  830: 
  831:   const computed = computeTop3({
  832:     lotteryKey,
  833:     drawsRange: history,
  834:     drawLast,
  835:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  836:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  837:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  838:     topN: 3,
  839:     targetYmdOverride: date,
  840:     targetHourOverride: closeHour,
  841:   });
  842: 
  843:   const predictions = mapTop3ToPredictions(
  844:     computed?.top
  845:   );
  846: 
  847:   const publicSnapshot =
  848:     buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
  849:       computedTop: computed?.top,
  850:       history,
  851:       lotteryKey,
  852:       date,
  853:       closeHour,
  854:       publicApi,
  855:     });
  856: 
  857:   if (!predictions.length) {
  858:     throw new Error(
  859:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu previsões válidas."
  860:     );
  861:   }
  862: 
  863:   if (
  864:     publicSnapshot.length !== 3 ||
  865:     publicSnapshot.some(
  866:       (item) =>
  867:         !Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ||
  868:         item.milhares20.length !== 20
  869:     )
  870:   ) {
  871:     throw new Error(
  872:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu 20 milhares válidas para cada grupo."
  873:     );
  874:   }
  875: 
  876:   const metadata = {
  877:     ...(input.metadata || {}),
  878:     engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
  879:     historyDraws: history.length,
  880:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
  881:     historySource,
  882:     historyBootstrapStatus:
  883:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
  884:     historyTotalStored:
  885:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
  886:     lookbackDays,
  887:     maxDraws,
  888:     startYmd,
  889:     lastDrawYmd:
  890:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || null,
  891:     lastDrawHour:
  892:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(drawLast) || null,
  893:     targetYmd: date,
  894:     targetHour: closeHour,
  895:     engineMeta: computed?.meta || null,
  896:   };
  897: 
  898:   const engine = {
  899:     name: "top3_statistical_v3",
  900:     historyDraws: history.length,
  901:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
  902:     historySource,
  903:     historyBootstrapStatus:
  904:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
  905:     historyTotalStored:
  906:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
  907:     lookbackDays,
  908:     maxDraws,
  909:     startYmd,
  910:     targetYmd: date,
  911:     targetHour: closeHour,
  912:     meta: computed?.meta || null,
  913:   };
  914: 
  915:   if (input.dryRun === true) {
  916:     return {
  917:       run: null,
  918:       predictions,
  919:       publicSnapshot,
  920:       engine,
  921:       dryRun: true,
  922:     };
  923:   }
  924: 
  925:   const source =
  926:     input.source || "backend-top3";
  927: 
  928:   const result = await persistRun({
  929:     lotteryKey,
  930:     date,
  931:     closeHour,
  932:     source,
  933:     algorithm: "top3_statistical_v3",
  934:     metadata,
  935:     predictions,
  936:   });
  937: 
  938:   const publicProjection =
  939:     await saveTop3PublicProjection({
  940:       lotteryKey,
  941:       date,
  942:       closeHour,
  943:       snapshot: publicSnapshot,
  944:       engineVersion: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  945:       source,
  946:     });
  947: 
  948:   return {
  949:     ...result,
  950:     publicSnapshot,
  951:     publicProjection,
  952:     engine,
  953:     dryRun: false,
  954:   };
  955: }
  956: 
  957: module.exports = {
  958:   createTop3PredictionRun,
  959:   mapTop3ToPredictions,
  960:   normalizeHour,
  961:   normalizeHistorySource,
  962:   loadPredictionHistory,
  963:   buildPublicMilharesCols,
  964:   buildTop3PublicSnapshot,
  965:   saveTop3PublicProjection,
  966:   resolveNextTop3Slot,
  967: };

========================================================================================================================
backend/engine/scoreEngineV2.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const scoreConfig = require("./scoreConfig");
    4: const { collectEvidence } = require("./evidenceEngine");
    5: 
    6: /**
    7:  * Score Engine V2 inicial
    8:  *
    9:  * Consome evidências.
   10:  * Não aplica regra cega isolada.
   11:  */
   12: 
   13: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
   14:   const n = Number(value);
   15:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
   16:   return n;
   17: }
   18: 
   19: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
   20:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
   21: 
   22:   if (!list.length) {
   23:     return {
   24:       score: 0,
   25:       confidence: 0,
   26:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
   27:       signals: {},
   28:     };
   29:   }
   30: 
   31:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
   32:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
   33:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
   34: 
   35:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
   36:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
   37: 
   38:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
   39: 
   40:   const signals = {};
   41:   for (const e of list) {
   42:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   return {
   46:     score,
   47:     confidence,
   48:     reasons,
   49:     signals,
   50:   };
   51: }
   52: 
   53: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
   54:   const collected = collectEvidence({
   55:     item,
   56:     context,
   57:     config: scoreConfig,
   58:   });
   59: 
   60:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
   61: 
   62:   return {
   63:     ...item,
   64:     score: strength.score,
   65:     confidence: strength.confidence,
   66:     reasons: strength.reasons,
   67:     signals: strength.signals,
   68:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
   69:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
   70:   };
   71: }
   72: 
   73: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
   74: 
   75:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
   76:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
   77:     .sort((a, b) => {
   78: 
   79:       if (b.score !== a.score)
   80:         return b.score - a.score;
   81: 
   82:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
   83:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
   84: 
   85:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
   86: 
   87:     });
   88: 
   89: }
   90: 
   91: module.exports = {
   92:   scoreItem,
   93:   scoreRanking,
   94:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
   95: };

========================================================================================================================
backend/engine/evidenceEngine.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const { buildFrequencyEvidence } = require("./frequencyEvidence");
    4: const { buildContextEvidence } = require("./contextEvidence");
    5: 
    6: /**
    7:  * Evidence Engine
    8:  *
    9:  * Centraliza todos os módulos de evidência.
   10:  * Cada módulo produz fatos; nenhum decide o resultado.
   11:  */
   12: 
   13: const MODULES = {
   14:   frequency: buildFrequencyEvidence,
   15:   context: buildContextEvidence,
   16: };
   17: 
   18: function collectEvidence({
   19:   item = {},
   20:   context = {},
   21:   config = {},
   22: } = {}) {
   23: 
   24:   const evidence = [];
   25:   const enabled = config.evidenceModules || {};
   26: 
   27:   for (const [name, builder] of Object.entries(MODULES)) {
   28: 
   29:     if (!enabled[name]?.enabled) {
   30:       continue;
   31:     }
   32: 
   33:     try {
   34: 
   35:       const result = builder(item, context);
   36: 
   37:       if (result) {
   38:         evidence.push(result);
   39:       }
   40: 
   41:     } catch (err) {
   42: 
   43:       evidence.push({
   44:         module: name,
   45:         error: true,
   46:         message: err.message,
   47:       });
   48: 
   49:     }
   50: 
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   return {
   54: 
   55:     count: evidence.filter(e => !e.error).length,
   56: 
   57:     modules: evidence
   58:       .filter(e => !e.error)
   59:       .map(e => e.module),
   60: 
   61:     evidence,
   62: 
   63:   };
   64: 
   65: }
   66: 
   67: module.exports = {
   68:   collectEvidence,
   69: };

backend/engine/contextEvidence.js

    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: /**
    4:  * Context Evidence V2
    5:  *
    6:  * Responsabilidade:
    7:  * Produzir evidências do contexto do sorteio.
    8:  * Não calcula score.
    9:  */
   10: 
   11: function normalize(value) {
   12:     const n = Number(value);
   13:     return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: function buildReasons(ctx) {
   17: 
   18:     const reasons = [];
   19: 
   20:     reasons.push("Contexto histórico identificado.");
   21: 
   22:     if (ctx.lotteryKey)
   23:         reasons.push(`Loteria: ${ctx.lotteryKey}`);
   24: 
   25:     if (ctx.weekday)
   26:         reasons.push(`Dia da semana: ${ctx.weekday}`);
   27: 
   28:     if (ctx.closeHour)
   29:         reasons.push(`Horário: ${ctx.closeHour}`);
   30: 
   31:     if (ctx.previousGroup != null)
   32:         reasons.push(`Grupo anterior: ${ctx.previousGroup}`);
   33: 
   34:     if (ctx.previousAnimal)
   35:         reasons.push(`Animal anterior: ${ctx.previousAnimal}`);
   36: 
   37:     return reasons;
   38: }
   39: 
   40: function buildContextEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   41: 
   42:     const evidence = {
   43: 
   44:         lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
   45: 
   46:         ymd: context.ymd || null,
   47: 
   48:         weekday: context.weekday || null,
   49: 
   50:         closeHour: context.hour || null,
   51: 
   52:         prize: normalize(context.prize),
   53: 
   54:         previousAnimal: context.previousAnimal || null,
   55: 
   56:         previousGroup: normalize(context.previousGroup),
   57: 
   58:         previousHour: context.previousHour || null,
   59: 
   60:         lookback: normalize(context.lookback),
   61: 
   62:         window: normalize(context.window),
   63: 
   64:     };
   65: 
   66:     return {
   67: 
   68:         module: "context",
   69: 
   70:         value: 1,
   71: 
   72:         evidence,
   73: 
   74:         reasons: buildReasons(evidence),
   75: 
   76:     };
   77: 
   78: }
   79: 
   80: module.exports = {
   81: 
   82:     buildContextEvidence,
   83: 
   84: };

========================================================================================================================
backend/engine/frequencyEvidence.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: \
    2: "use strict";
    3: 
    4: /**
    5:  * Frequency Evidence V2
    6:  *
    7:  * Responsabilidade:
    8:  * Produzir evidências relacionadas à frequência.
    9:  * Não calcula score.
   10:  */
   11: 
   12: function normalize(value) {
   13:   const n = Number(value);
   14: 
   15:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
   16:     return 0;
   17:   }
   18: 
   19:   return n;
   20: }
   21: 
   22: function normalizePercent(value) {
   23:   const n = Number(value);
   24: 
   25:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
   26:     return 0;
   27:   }
   28: 
   29:   return Math.min(100, n);
   30: }
   31: 
   32: function classifyFrequency(percent) {
   33:   if (percent >= 70) return "very_high";
   34:   if (percent >= 50) return "high";
   35:   if (percent >= 30) return "medium";
   36:   if (percent >= 15) return "low";
   37:   return "very_low";
   38: }
   39: 
   40: function buildReasons(frequency, percent, level) {
   41:   const reasons = [];
   42: 
   43:   reasons.push(`Frequência histórica: ${frequency}`);
   44: 
   45:   if (percent > 0) {
   46:     reasons.push(`Participação: ${percent.toFixed(1)}%`);
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   switch (level) {
   50:     case "very_high":
   51:       reasons.push("Frequência muito elevada.");
   52:       break;
   53: 
   54:     case "high":
   55:       reasons.push("Frequência elevada.");
   56:       break;
   57: 
   58:     case "medium":
   59:       reasons.push("Frequência intermediária.");
   60:       break;
   61: 
   62:     case "low":
   63:       reasons.push("Frequência baixa.");
   64:       break;
   65: 
   66:     default:
   67:       reasons.push("Pouca evidência de frequência.");
   68:   }
   69: 
   70:   return reasons;
   71: }
   72: 
   73: function buildFrequencyEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   74:   const frequency = normalize(
   75:     item.frequency ??
   76:     item.total ??
   77:     item.count ??
   78:     item.appearances
   79:   );
   80: 
   81:   const totalUniverse = normalize(
   82:     context.totalDraws ??
   83:     context.windowSize ??
   84:     context.sampleSize
   85:   );
   86: 
   87:   const percent =
   88:     totalUniverse > 0
   89:       ? normalizePercent((frequency / totalUniverse) * 100)
   90:       : normalizePercent(
   91:           item.frequencyPercent ??
   92:           item.frequencyRate
   93:         );
   94: 
   95:   const level = classifyFrequency(percent);
   96: 
   97:   return {
   98:     module: "frequency",
   99: 
  100:     value: percent,
  101: 
  102:     evidence: {
  103:       frequency,
  104:       percent,
  105:       level,
  106:       totalUniverse,
  107: 
  108:       window: context.window || null,
  109:       lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
  110:       position: context.position || null,
  111:       weekday: context.weekday || null,
  112:       hour: context.hour || null,
  113:     },
  114: 
  115:     reasons: buildReasons(frequency, percent, level),
  116:   };
  117: }
  118: 
  119: module.exports = {
  120:   buildFrequencyEvidence,
  121: };

backend/engine/predictionModel.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: /**
    4:  * Modelo central do Score Engine.
    5:  * Ainda não calcula nada. Só padroniza o formato das previsões.
    6:  */
    7: 
    8: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    9:   const s = String(value || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   10:   if (s === "RJ" || s === "RIO" || s === "PT-RIO" || s === "PT_RIO") return "PT_RIO";
   11:   if (s === "FED" || s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   12:   return s || "PT_RIO";
   13: }
   14: 
   15: function pad2(n) {
   16:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   17: }
   18: 
   19: function makePredictionRunId({ lotteryKey, date, closeHour, source = "manual" }) {
   20:   const lk = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
   21:   const d = String(date || "").trim();
   22:   const h = String(closeHour || "").trim().replace(":", "");
   23:   const src = String(source || "manual").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "_");
   24: 
   25:   return `${lk}__${d}__${h || "NA"}__${src}`;
   26: }
   27: 
   28: function buildPredictionRun({
   29:   lotteryKey,
   30:   date,
   31:   closeHour,
   32:   source = "manual",
   33:   algorithm = "score_engine_v1",
   34:   status = "generated",
   35:   metadata = {},
   36: } = {}) {
   37:   const lk = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
   38: 
   39:   return {
   40:     id: makePredictionRunId({ lotteryKey: lk, date, closeHour, source }),
   41:     lottery_key: lk,
   42:     date: String(date || "").trim(),
   43:     ymd: String(date || "").trim(),
   44:     close_hour: String(closeHour || "").trim() || null,
   45:     source,
   46:     algorithm,
   47:     status,
   48:     metadata,
   49:     createdAt: null,
   50:     updatedAt: null,
   51:   };
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function buildPrediction({
   55:   runId,
   56:   type = "grupo",
   57:   grupo,
   58:   animal,
   59:   dezena = null,
   60:   centena = null,
   61:   milhar = null,
   62:   score = 0,
   63:   confidence = 0,
   64:   reasons = [],
   65:   signals = {},
   66: } = {}) {
   67:   const g = Number(grupo);
   68:   const grupo2 = Number.isFinite(g) ? pad2(g) : null;
   69: 
   70:   return {
   71:     runId,
   72:     type,
   73:     grupo: grupo2,
   74:     animal: animal || null,
   75:     dezena,
   76:     centena,
   77:     milhar,
   78:     score: Number(score) || 0,
   79:     confidence: Number(confidence) || 0,
   80:     reasons: Array.isArray(reasons) ? reasons : [],
   81:     signals: signals && typeof signals === "object" ? signals : {},
   82:     status: "pending",
   83:     result: null,
   84:     createdAt: null,
   85:     updatedAt: null,
   86:   };
   87: }
   88: 
   89: module.exports = {
   90:   normalizeLotteryKey,
   91:   makePredictionRunId,
   92:   buildPredictionRun,
   93:   buildPrediction,
   94: };

backend/engine/top3HistoryRepository.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const {
    4:   getDb,
    5: } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
    6: 
    7: const ROOT_COLLECTION = "top3_history";
    8: const MONTHS_COLLECTION = "months";
    9: const METADATA_COLLECTION = "metadata";
   10: const CURRENT_METADATA_DOC = "current";
   11: const SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
   12: 
   13: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
   14:   const key = String(value || "PT_RIO")
   15:     .trim()
   16:     .toUpperCase();
   17: 
   18:   if (!key) {
   19:     throw new Error(
   20:       "lotteryKey obrigatório."
   21:     );
   22:   }
   23: 
   24:   return key;
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function normalizeYearMonth(value) {
   28:   const yearMonth = String(value || "").trim();
   29: 
   30:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(yearMonth)) {
   31:     throw new Error(
   32:       "yearMonth inválido. Utilize YYYY-MM."
   33:     );
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   const month = Number(yearMonth.slice(5, 7));
   37: 
   38:   if (month < 1 || month > 12) {
   39:     throw new Error(
   40:       "Mês inválido em yearMonth."
   41:     );
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   return yearMonth;
   45: }
   46: 
   47: function normalizeYmd(value) {
   48:   const ymd = String(value || "").trim();
   49: 
   50:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(ymd)) {
   51:     return null;
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   return ymd;
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function resolveDb(dependencies = {}) {
   58:   if (dependencies.db) {
   59:     return dependencies.db;
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   return getDb();
   63: }
   64: 
   65: function historyRootRef(
   66:   database,
   67:   lotteryKey
   68: ) {
   69:   return database
   70:     .collection(ROOT_COLLECTION)
   71:     .doc(normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey));
   72: }
   73: 
   74: function historyMonthRef(
   75:   database,
   76:   lotteryKey,
   77:   yearMonth
   78: ) {
   79:   return historyRootRef(
   80:     database,
   81:     lotteryKey
   82:   )
   83:     .collection(MONTHS_COLLECTION)
   84:     .doc(normalizeYearMonth(yearMonth));
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function metadataRef(
   88:   database,
   89:   lotteryKey
   90: ) {
   91:   return historyRootRef(
   92:     database,
   93:     lotteryKey
   94:   )
   95:     .collection(METADATA_COLLECTION)
   96:     .doc(CURRENT_METADATA_DOC);
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function safeArray(value) {
  100:   return Array.isArray(value)
  101:     ? value
  102:     : [];
  103: }
  104: 
  105: function normalizePrize(prize = {}) {
  106:   const position = Number(prize.position);
  107:   const grupo = Number(prize.grupo);
  108: 
  109:   if (
  110:     !Number.isFinite(position) ||
  111:     position < 1 ||
  112:     position > 10 ||
  113:     !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
  114:     grupo < 1 ||
  115:     grupo > 25
  116:   ) {
  117:     return null;
  118:   }
  119: 
  120:   return {
  121:     id: prize.id
  122:       ? String(prize.id)
  123:       : null,
  124:     position,
  125:     grupo,
  126:     milhar:
  127:       prize.milhar != null
  128:         ? String(prize.milhar).padStart(4, "0")
  129:         : null,
  130:     centena:
  131:       prize.centena != null
  132:         ? String(prize.centena).padStart(3, "0")
  133:         : null,
  134:     dezena:
  135:       prize.dezena != null
  136:         ? String(prize.dezena).padStart(2, "0")
  137:         : null,
  138:   };
  139: }
  140: 
  141: function normalizeDraw(draw = {}) {
  142:   const drawId = String(
  143:     draw.drawId ||
  144:     draw.id ||
  145:     ""
  146:   ).trim();
  147: 
  148:   const ymd = normalizeYmd(
  149:     draw.ymd ||
  150:     draw.date
  151:   );
  152: 
  153:   const closeHour = String(
  154:     draw.close_hour ||
  155:     draw.closeHour ||
  156:     draw.hour ||
  157:     ""
  158:   ).trim();
  159: 
  160:   if (!drawId || !ymd || !closeHour) {
  161:     return null;
  162:   }
  163: 
  164:   const prizes = safeArray(draw.prizes)
  165:     .map(normalizePrize)
  166:     .filter(Boolean)
  167:     .sort(
  168:       (a, b) =>
  169:         Number(a.position) -
  170:         Number(b.position)
  171:     );
  172: 
  173:   return {
  174:     drawId,
  175:     id: drawId,
  176:     ymd,
  177:     closeHour,
  178:     lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(
  179:       draw.lottery_key ||
  180:       draw.lotteryKey ||
  181:       "PT_RIO"
  182:     ),
  183:     lotteryCode:
  184:       draw.lottery_code ||
  185:       draw.lotteryCode ||
  186:       null,
  187:     prizes,
  188:   };
  189: }
  190: 
  191: function sortDraws(draws = []) {
  192:   return [...draws].sort((a, b) => {
  193:     const aKey =
  194:       `${a.ymd}T${a.closeHour}T${a.drawId}`;
  195: 
  196:     const bKey =
  197:       `${b.ymd}T${b.closeHour}T${b.drawId}`;
  198: 
  199:     return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
  200:   });
  201: }
  202: 
  203: function deduplicateDraws(draws = []) {
  204:   const map = new Map();
  205: 
  206:   for (const raw of safeArray(draws)) {
  207:     const draw = normalizeDraw(raw);
  208: 
  209:     if (!draw) {
  210:       continue;
  211:     }
  212: 
  213:     map.set(draw.drawId, draw);
  214:   }
  215: 
  216:   return sortDraws(
  217:     Array.from(map.values())
  218:   );
  219: }
  220: 
  221: async function readHistoryMonth(
  222:   lotteryKey,
  223:   yearMonth,
  224:   dependencies = {}
  225: ) {
  226:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
  227: 
  228:   const snap = await historyMonthRef(
  229:     database,
  230:     lotteryKey,
  231:     yearMonth
  232:   ).get();
  233: 
  234:   if (!snap.exists) {
  235:     return {
  236:       exists: false,
  237:       lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(
  238:         lotteryKey
  239:       ),
  240:       yearMonth: normalizeYearMonth(
  241:         yearMonth
  242:       ),
  243:       draws: [],
  244:       data: null,
  245:     };
  246:   }
  247: 
  248:   const data = snap.data() || {};
  249: 
  250:   return {
  251:     exists: true,
  252:     lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(
  253:       lotteryKey
  254:     ),
  255:     yearMonth: normalizeYearMonth(
  256:       yearMonth
  257:     ),
  258:     draws: deduplicateDraws(data.draws),
  259:     data,
  260:   };
  261: }
  262: 
  263: async function writeHistoryMonth(
  264:   lotteryKey,
  265:   yearMonth,
  266:   draws,
  267:   dependencies = {}
  268: ) {
  269:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
  270:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
  271:   const month = normalizeYearMonth(yearMonth);
  272: 
  273:   const normalizedDraws = deduplicateDraws(
  274:     draws
  275:   ).filter(
  276:     (draw) =>
  277:       draw.ymd.slice(0, 7) === month
  278:   );
  279: 
  280:   const firstDraw =
  281:     normalizedDraws[0] || null;
  282: 
  283:   const lastDraw =
  284:     normalizedDraws[
  285:       normalizedDraws.length - 1
  286:     ] || null;
  287: 
  288:   const payload = {
  289:     schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,
  290:     lotteryKey: key,
  291:     yearMonth: month,
  292:     drawCount: normalizedDraws.length,
  293:     firstYmd: firstDraw?.ymd || null,
  294:     lastYmd: lastDraw?.ymd || null,
  295:     firstDrawId:
  296:       firstDraw?.drawId || null,
  297:     lastDrawId:
  298:       lastDraw?.drawId || null,
  299:     draws: normalizedDraws,
  300:     updatedAt: new Date(),
  301:   };
  302: 
  303:   await historyMonthRef(
  304:     database,
  305:     key,
  306:     month
  307:   ).set(
  308:     payload,
  309:     {
  310:       merge: false,
  311:     }
  312:   );
  313: 
  314:   return payload;
  315: }
  316: 
  317: async function upsertHistoryMonth(
  318:   lotteryKey,
  319:   yearMonth,
  320:   newDraws,
  321:   dependencies = {}
  322: ) {
  323:   const current = await readHistoryMonth(
  324:     lotteryKey,
  325:     yearMonth,
  326:     dependencies
  327:   );
  328: 
  329:   const merged = deduplicateDraws([
  330:     ...current.draws,
  331:     ...safeArray(newDraws),
  332:   ]);
  333: 
  334:   return writeHistoryMonth(
  335:     lotteryKey,
  336:     yearMonth,
  337:     merged,
  338:     dependencies
  339:   );
  340: }
  341: 
  342: async function readMetadata(
  343:   lotteryKey,
  344:   dependencies = {}
  345: ) {
  346:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
  347:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
  348: 
  349:   const snap = await metadataRef(
  350:     database,
  351:     key
  352:   ).get();
  353: 
  354:   if (!snap.exists) {
  355:     return {
  356:       exists: false,
  357:       lotteryKey: key,
  358:       data: null,
  359:     };
  360:   }
  361: 
  362:   return {
  363:     exists: true,
  364:     lotteryKey: key,
  365:     data: snap.data() || {},
  366:   };
  367: }
  368: 
  369: async function writeMetadata(
  370:   lotteryKey,
  371:   metadata = {},
  372:   dependencies = {}
  373: ) {
  374:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
  375:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
  376: 
  377:   const payload = {
  378:     ...metadata,
  379:     schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,
  380:     lotteryKey: key,
  381:     updatedAt: new Date(),
  382:   };
  383: 
  384:   await metadataRef(
  385:     database,
  386:     key
  387:   ).set(
  388:     payload,
  389:     {
  390:       merge: true,
  391:     }
  392:   );
  393: 
  394:   return payload;
  395: }
  396: 
  397: async function listHistoryMonths(
  398:   lotteryKey,
  399:   dependencies = {}
  400: ) {
  401:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
  402:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
  403: 
  404:   const snap = await historyRootRef(
  405:     database,
  406:     key
  407:   )
  408:     .collection(MONTHS_COLLECTION)
  409:     .get();
  410: 
  411:   return snap.docs
  412:     .map((doc) => ({
  413:       id: doc.id,
  414:       ...(doc.data() || {}),
  415:     }))
  416:     .sort(
  417:       (a, b) =>
  418:         String(a.yearMonth || a.id)
  419:           .localeCompare(
  420:             String(b.yearMonth || b.id)
  421:           )
  422:     );
  423: }
  424: 
  425: async function readFullHistory(
  426:   lotteryKey,
  427:   dependencies = {}
  428: ) {
  429:   const months = await listHistoryMonths(
  430:     lotteryKey,
  431:     dependencies
  432:   );
  433: 
  434:   const draws = [];
  435: 
  436:   for (const month of months) {
  437:     draws.push(
  438:       ...safeArray(month.draws)
  439:     );
  440:   }
  441: 
  442:   return deduplicateDraws(draws);
  443: }
  444: 
  445: module.exports = {
  446:   ROOT_COLLECTION,
  447:   MONTHS_COLLECTION,
  448:   METADATA_COLLECTION,
  449:   SCHEMA_VERSION,
  450:   normalizeLotteryKey,
  451:   normalizeYearMonth,
  452:   normalizeDraw,
  453:   deduplicateDraws,
  454:   readHistoryMonth,
  455:   writeHistoryMonth,
  456:   upsertHistoryMonth,
  457:   readMetadata,
  458:   writeMetadata,
  459:   listHistoryMonths,
  460:   readFullHistory,
  461: };

   32: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
   33:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   34:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
   35:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
   36: 
   37:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
   38: 
   39:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
   40: 
   41:   return list
   42:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
   43:     .map((draw) => {
   44:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   45:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   46:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
   47:     })
   48:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
   49:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
   50: }
   51: 
   52: /* =========================
   53:    Draw helpers (robustos)
   54: ========================= */
   55: 
   56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
   57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
   58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
   59: 
   60:   const n = Number(s);
   61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
   62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
   63: 
   64:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
   65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
   66: }
   67: 
   68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   72: 
   73:   let score = 0;
   74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   75:   if (h) score += 20;
   76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   77: 
   78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   80: 
   81:   return score;
   82: }
   83: 
   84: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
   85:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
   86:     ? Number(p.position)
   87:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
   88:       ? Number(p.posicao)
   89:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
   90:         ? Number(p.pos)
   91:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
   92:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
   93:           : null;
   94: 
   95:   return pos;
   96: }
   97: 
   98: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
   99:   const directCandidates = [
  100:     p?.grupo2,
  101:     p?.group2,
  102:     p?.grupo,
  103:     p?.group,
  104:     p?.animal_grupo,
  105:     p?.grupo_animal,
  106:     p?.grupoAnimal,
  107:     p?.g,
  108:   ];
  109: 
  110:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
  111:     const g = Number(v);
  112:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
  113:       return g;
  114:     }
  115:   }
  116: 
  117:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
  118: 
  119:   if (milhar4) {
  120:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
  121:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
  122: 
  123:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
  124:   }
  125: 
  126:   return null;
  127: }
  128: 
  129: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {
  130:   return normalizeHourLike(
  131:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
  132:   );
  133: }
  134: 
  135: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
  136:   const y =
  137:     draw?.ymd ||
  138:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
  139:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
  140:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
  141:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
  142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
  143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
  144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
  145:     null;
  146: 
  147:   return y;
  148: }
  149: 
  150: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
  151:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  152:   if (prizes.length) {
  153:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
  154:     if (p1) {
  155:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
  156:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
  157:     }
  158:   }
  159: 
  160:   const candidates = [
  161:     draw?.grupo1,
  162:     draw?.group1,
  163:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
  164:     draw?.first_grupo,
  165:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
  166:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
  167:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
  168:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
  169:     draw?.g1,
  170:     draw?.grupo_1,
  171:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
  172:   ];
  173: 
  174:   for (const v of candidates) {
  175:     if (Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return Number(v);
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   return null;
  179: }

  181: /* =========================
  182:    Helpers internos
  183: ========================= */
  184: 
  185: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
  186:   const n = Number(v);
  187:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
  188: }
  189: 
  190: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
  191:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
  192:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
  193: }
  194: 
  195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
  196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
  197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
  198: 
  199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  200: 
  201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
  202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
  203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  204:   }
  205: 
  206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
  207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
  208: 
  209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
  210: }

  212: /* =========================
  213:    Schedules
  214: ========================= */
  215: 
  216: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
  217:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
  218:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
  219: }
  220: 
  221: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
  222:   ymd,
  223:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  224:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
  225: ) {
  226:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
  227:   if (dow === 0) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY;
  228:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT;
  229:   return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL;
  230: }
  231: 
  232: export function getScheduleForLottery({
  233:   lotteryKey,
  234:   ymd,
  235:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  236:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  237:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  238: }) {
  239:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  240: 
  241:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  242:     return isFederalDrawDay(ymd) ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE : [];
  243:   }
  244: 
  245:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
  246:     ymd,
  247:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  248:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
  249:   );
  250: }
  251: 
  252: export function scheduleSet(schedule) {
  253:   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));
  254: }
  255: 
  256: export function isHourInSchedule(schedule, hhmm) {
  257:   const s = scheduleSet(schedule);
  258:   return s.has(toHourBucket(hhmm));
  259: }
  260: 
  261: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
  262:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  263:   if (!list.length) return null;
  264: 
  265:   const sorted = [...list]
  266:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  267:     .sort((a, b) => {
  268:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  269:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  270:       return tb - ta;
  271:     });
  272: 
  273:   return sorted[0] || null;
  274: }
  275: 
  276: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
  277:   draws,
  278:   targetHourBucket,
  279:   schedule
  280: ) {
  281:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  282:   if (!list.length) return null;
  283: 
  284:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
  285:   if (t < 0) return null;
  286: 
  287:   const candidates = list
  288:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  289:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
  290:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
  291:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  292: 
  293:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
  294: }
