 2659:         `Grupo G${g2}: duplicação histórica=${(x.probDuplication * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2660:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ordem 2=${(x.probSeq2 * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2661:         `Grupo G${g2}: par do dia=${(x.probPair * 100).toFixed(2)}% | confiança=${(x.pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2662:         `Grupo G${g2}: boost de repetição=${(x.repeatBoost * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2663:         `Grupo G${g2}: memória curta=${(x.probMemory * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2664:         `Grupo G${g2}: recência composta=${(x.recentComposite * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2665:         `Grupo G${g2}: atraso normalizado=${(x.lateNorm * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2666:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs na camada=${x.condFirstCount}`,
 2667:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs estruturais=${x.structuralFirstCount}`,
 2668:         `Grupo G${g2}: TOP3 estruturais=${x.structuralPrizePresenceCount}`,
 2669:         `Grupo G${g2}: draws com duplicação=${x.duplicationCount}`,
 2670:         `Grupo G${g2}: peso de memória=${Number(x.memoryWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
 2671:         `Grupo G${g2}: frequência no dia=${x.dayFreq}`,
 2672:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs no dia=${x.dayFirstFreq}`,
 2673:         `Grupo G${g2}: dominante no dia=${x.isDominantToday ? "sim" : "não"}`,
 2674:         `Grupo G${g2}: score do fluxo do dia=${Number(x.dayFlowScore || 0).toFixed(2)} | confiança=${(Number(x.dayFlowConfidence || 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
 2675:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | amostras=${x.pairSamples} | amostras ponderadas=${Number(x.pairWeightedSamples || 0).toFixed(2)} | DOM exato=${x.pairExactDomSamples} | ocorrências=${Number(x.pairCount || 0).toFixed(2)} | prob=${(Number(x.pairProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}% | score=${Number(x.pairSequenceScore || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
 2676:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 2677:       ],
 2678:       meta: {
 2679:         trigger: {
 2680:           ymd: lastY,
 2681:           hour: lastH,
 2682:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2683:         },
 2684:         next: {
 2685:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 2686:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
 2687:         },
 2688:         samples: condSamples,
 2689:         scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
 2690:         explain: {
 2691:           engine: "V2_BICHO",
 2692:           mode,
 2693:           regime,
 2694:           repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
 2695:           uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
 2696:           weights: adjustedWeights,
 2697:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 2698:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
 2699:           layerSamples: condSamples,
 2700:           structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
 2701:           structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
 2702:           duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
 2703:           memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
 2704:           memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
 2705:           seq2Samples: Number(seq2Out?.samples || 0),
 2706:           seq2Prev2: Number(seq2Out?.prev2 || 0),
 2707:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),
 2708:           pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
 2709:           pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
 2710:           pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
 2711:           pairConfidence,
 2712:           repeatWindowSize: Number(repeatBoostOut?.windowSize || 0),
 2713:           repeatWindowConsidered: Number(repeatBoostOut?.considered || 0),
 2714:           currentState,
 2715:           targetDow,
 2716:           targetDayOfMonth,
 2717:           prevHour: lastH,
 2718:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2719:           dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
 2720:           dayFlowConfidence,
 2721:           dominanceScore: x.dominanceScore,
 2722:           recentComposite: x.recentComposite,
 2723:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
 2724:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
 2725:           recentDupDraws: x.recentDupDraws,
 2726:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
 2727:             key: layer.key,
 2728:             label: layer.label,
 2729:             samples: layer.samples,
 2730:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 2731:           })),
 2732:         },
 2733:       },
 2734:     };
 2735:   });
 2736: 
 2737:   return {
 2738:     top,
 2739:     meta: {
 2740:       trigger: {
 2741:         ymd: lastY,
 2742:         hour: lastH,
 2743:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2744:       },
 2745:       next: {
 2746:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 2747:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
 2748:       },
 2749:       samples: condSamples,
 2750:       scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
 2751:       explain: {
 2752:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
 2753:         mode,
 2754:         regime,
 2755:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
 2756:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
 2757:         weights: adjustedWeights,
 2758:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 2759:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
 2760:         layerSamples: condSamples,
 2761:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
 2762:         structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
 2763:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
 2764:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
 2765:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
 2766:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
 2767:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
 2768:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
 2769:         pairConfidence,
 2770:         currentState,
 2771:         targetDow,
 2772:         targetDayOfMonth,
 2773:         prevHour: lastH,
 2774:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2775:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
 2776:         dayFlowConfidence,
 2777:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
 2778:           key: layer.key,
 2779:           label: layer.label,
 2780:           samples: layer.samples,
 2781:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 2782:         })),
 2783:       },
 2784:     },
 2785:   };
 2786: }
 2787: 
 2788: 
 2789: /* =========================
 2790:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
 2791: ========================= */
 2792: 
 2793: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
 2794:   const k = Number(key);
 2795:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
 2796:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
 2797: }
 2798: 
 2799: function emptyGroupMap() {
 2800:   const out = new Map();
 2801:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
 2802:   return out;
 2803: }
 2804: 
 2805: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
 2806:   return new Set(
 2807:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
 2808:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 2809:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
 2810:   );
 2811: }
 2812: 
 2813: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
 2814:   const out = new Map();
 2815:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
 2816:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
 2817:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
 2818:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
 2819: 
 2820:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2821:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
 2822:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
 2823:   }
 2824: 
 2825:   return out;
 2826: }
 2827: 
 2828: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
 2829:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
 2830:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
 2831:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
 2832: }
 2833: 
 2834: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
 2835:   lotteryKey,
 2836:   drawsRange,
 2837:   drawLast,
 2838:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2839:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2840:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2841:   topN = 3,
 2842:   targetYmdOverride = "",
 2843:   targetHourOverride = "",
 2844: }) {
 2845:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
 2846:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
 2847: 
 2848:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 2849: 
 2850:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
 2851:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
 2852:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
 2853: 
 2854:   if (
 2855:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
 2856:     !lastH ||
 2857:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
 2858:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
 2859:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2860:   ) {
 2861:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 2862:   }
 2863: 
 2864:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
 2865:     lotteryKey: key,
 2866:     ymd: lastY,
 2867:     hourBucket: lastH,
 2868:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2869:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2870:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2871:   });
 2872: 
 2873:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
 2874:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
 2875: 
 2876:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 2877:     ? forcedTargetY
 2878:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
 2879: 
 2880:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 2881:     ? forcedTargetH
 2882:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
 2883:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
 2884:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
 2885:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
 2886: 
 2887:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
 2888:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 2889:   }
 2890: 
 2891:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
 2892:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 2893:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 2894:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 2895:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
 2896:   });
 2897: 
 2898:   const layers = {
 2899:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
 2900:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
 2901:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
 2902:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
 2903:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
 2904:   };
 2905: 
 2906:   for (const d of history) {
 2907:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 2908:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 2909:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
 2910:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
 2911:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 2912:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
 2913: 
 2914:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
 2915:       layer.samples += 1;
 2916:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
 2917:       for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layer.prizePresence, g, 1);
 2918:     };
 2919: 
 2920:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
 2921:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
 2922:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
 2923:   }
 2924: 
 2925:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
 2926: 
 2927:   for (const prev of history) {
 2928:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
 2929:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
 2930:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
 2931: 
 2932:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
 2933: 
 2934:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
 2935:       lotteryKey: key,
 2936:       ymd: py,
 2937:       hourBucket: ph,
 2938:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2939:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2940:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2941:     });
 2942: 
 2943:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
 2944:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
 2945: 
 2946:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
 2947:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
 2948: 
 2949:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
 2950:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(nextDraw);
 2951: 
 2952:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
 2953:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
 2954:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.transition.prizePresence, g, 1);
 2955:   }
 2956: 
 2957:   const recentComparable = history
 2958:     .filter((d) => {
 2959:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 2960:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 2961:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
 2962:     })
 2963:     .slice(-20);
 2964: 
 2965:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
 2966:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 2967:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
 2968: 
 2969:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
 2970:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
 2971:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.recent.prizePresence, g, 1);
 2972:   }
 2973: 
 2974:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
 2975:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
 2976:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
 2977:   const sceneWeight =
 2978:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
 2979:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
 2980: 
 2981: 
 2982:   const activeWeights = {};
 2983:   let totalWeight = 0;
 2984: 
 2985:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
 2986:     const conf =
 2987:       keyLayer === "transition"
 2988:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
 2989:         : keyLayer === "recent"
 2990:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
 2991:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
 2992: 
 2993:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
 2994:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
 2995:     totalWeight += w;
 2996:   }
 2997: 
 2998:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
 2999:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
 3000:     totalWeight = 1;
 3001:   }
 3002: 
 3003:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
 3004:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
 3005:   }
 3006: 
 3007:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
 3008:     const grupo = idx + 1;
 3009: 
 3010:     let scoreProb = 0;
 3011:     const details = {};
 3012: 
 3013:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
 3014:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
 3015:       const pPrizePresence = Number(layerProbability(layer.prizePresence, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
 3016: 
 3017:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.92) + (pPrizePresence * 0.08);
 3018:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
 3019: 
 3020:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
 3021: 
 3022:       details[keyLayer] = {
 3023:         label: layer.label,
 3024:         samples: layer.samples,
 3025:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
 3026:         top3Count: Number(layer.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0),
 3027:         probability: pLayer,
 3028:         weight: w,
 3029:       };
 3030:     }
 3031: 
 3032:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
 3033:     const pScene =
 3034:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
 3035:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
 3036: 
 3037:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
 3038:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
 3039:     }
 3040: 
 3041:     details.scene = {
 3042:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
 3043:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
 3044:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
 3045:       top3Count: 0,
 3046:       probability: pScene,
 3047:       weight: sceneWeight,
 3048:     };
 3049: 
 3050:     return {
 3051:       grupo,
 3052:       scoreProb,
 3053:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
 3054:       details,
 3055:     };
 3056:   })
 3057:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3058:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
 3059:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
 3060:     })
 3061:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
 3062: 
 3063:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
 3064:     ranked,
 3065:     {
 3066:       lotteryKey,
 3067:       targetYmd: targetY,
 3068:       targetHour: targetH,
 3069:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
 3070:       totalDraws: history.length,
 3071:     }
 3072:   );
 3073: 
 3074:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
 3075:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
 3076:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
 3077: 
 3078:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
 3079:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
 3080:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
 3081:       .slice(0, 3);
 3082: 
 3083:     return {
 3084:       rank: idx + 1,
 3085:       title:
 3086:         idx === 0
 3087:           ? "Mais provável"
 3088:           : idx === 1
 3089:             ? "2º mais provável"
 3090:             : "3º mais provável",
 3091:       grupo: x.grupo,
 3092:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
 3093:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
 3094:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
 3095:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
 3096:       lateBonus: 0,
 3097:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
 3098:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
 3099:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
 3100:       freqZeroWhy: "",
 3101:       reasons: [
 3102:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
 3103:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
 3104:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
 3105:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 3106:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
 3107:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
 3108:         ),
 3109:       ],
 3110:       meta: {
 3111:         trigger: {
 3112:           ymd: lastY,
 3113:           hour: lastH,
 3114:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3115:         },
 3116:         next: {
 3117:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 3118:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
 3119:         },
 3120:         samples: history.length,
 3121:         period: {
 3122:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
 3123:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
 3124:         },
 3125:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3126:         explain: {
 3127:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3128:           targetDow,
 3129:           targetDayOfMonth,
 3130:           prevHour: lastH,
 3131:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3132:           activeWeights,
 3133:           scene: {
 3134:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
 3135:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
 3136:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
 3137:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
 3138:           },
 3139:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
 3140:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
 3141:               k,
 3142:               {
 3143:                 label: v.label,
 3144:                 samples: v.samples,
 3145:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
 3146:               },
 3147:             ])
 3148:           ),
 3149:           details: x.details,
 3150:         },
 3151:       },
 3152:     };
 3153:   });
 3154: 
 3155:   return {
 3156:     top,
 3157:     meta: {
 3158:       trigger: {
 3159:         ymd: lastY,
 3160:         hour: lastH,
 3161:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3162:       },
 3163:       next: {
 3164:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 3165:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
 3166:       },
 3167:       samples: history.length,
 3168:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3169:       explain: {
 3170:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3171:         targetDow,
 3172:         targetDayOfMonth,
 3173:         prevHour: lastH,
 3174:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3175:         activeWeights,
 3176:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
 3177:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
 3178:             k,
 3179:             {
 3180:               label: v.label,
 3181:               samples: v.samples,
 3182:               weight: activeWeights[k],
 3183:             },
 3184:           ])
 3185:         ),
 3186:       },
 3187:     },
 3188:   };
 3189: }
 3190: 
 3191: 
 3192: 
 3193: /* =========================
 3194:    TOP3 SCENE READER
 3195: ========================= */
 3196: 
 3197: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
 3198:   const candidates = [
 3199:     prize?.milhar,
 3200:     prize?.milhar4,
 3201:     prize?.numero,
 3202:     prize?.number,
 3203:     prize?.value,
 3204:     prize?.result,
 3205:     prize?.resultado,
 3206:     prize?.premio,
 3207:   ];
 3208: 
 3209:   for (const v of candidates) {
 3210:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
 3211:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
 3212:   }
 3213: 
 3214:   return "";
 3215: }
 3216: 
 3217: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3218:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3219:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3220: 
 3221:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3222:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3223: 
 3224:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3225:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3226: 
 3227:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3228: }
 3229: 
 3230: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
 3231:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 3232: 
 3233:   const rows = prizes
 3234:     .map((p) => {
 3235:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
 3236:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
 3237:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
 3238: 
 3239:       return {
 3240:         pos,
 3241:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
 3242:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
 3243:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
 3244:         grupo,
 3245:       };
 3246:     })
 3247:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
 3248:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
 3249: 
 3250:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
 3251:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
 3252:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
 3253:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
 3254: 
 3255:   const countMap = (arr) => {
 3256:     const m = new Map();
 3257:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
 3258:     return Array.from(m.entries())
 3259:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
 3260:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
 3261:   };
 3262: 
 3263:   return {
 3264:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
 3265:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
 3266:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
 3267:     grupos,
 3268:     dezenas,
 3269:     centenas,
 3270:     milhares,
 3271:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
 3272:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
 3273:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
 3274:     rows,
 3275:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
 3276:   };
 3277: }
 3278: 
 3279: 
 3280: 
 3281: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
 3282:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
 3283: 
 3284:   let score = 0;
 3285:   const details = {};
 3286: 
 3287:   const add = (key, value) => {
 3288:     if (!value) return;
 3289:     score += value;
 3290:     details[key] = value;
 3291:   };
 3292: 
 3293:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
 3294:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
 3295: 
 3296:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
 3297:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
 3298: 
 3299:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
 3300:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
 3301: 
 3302:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
 3303:     add("firstGrupo",45);
 3304: 
 3305:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
 3306:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
 3307:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
 3308:   }
 3309: 
 3310:   add(
 3311:     "groupOverlap",
 3312:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
 3313:   );
 3314: 
 3315:   add(
 3316:     "dezenaOverlap",
 3317:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
 3318:   );
 3319: 
 3320:   add(
 3321:     "centenaOverlap",
 3322:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
 3323:   );
 3324: 
 3325:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3326:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3327: 
 3328:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
 3329:     add("repeatExists",8);
 3330: 
 3331:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
 3332:     add("repeatEqual",6);
 3333: 
 3334:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
 3335:     add("signature",60);
 3336: 
 3337:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
 3338:     add("hour",8);
 3339: 
 3340:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
 3341: }
 3342: 
 3343: 
 3344: 
 3345: 
 3346: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
 3347:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 3348:   if (!currentScene) return [];
 3349: 
 3350:   const ordered = list
 3351:     .map((draw) => ({
 3352:       draw,
 3353:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
 3354:     }))
 3355:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
 3356:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3357:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3358:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3359:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
 3360:     });
 3361: 
 3362:   return ordered
 3363:     .map((item, idx) => {
 3364:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
 3365:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
 3366: 
 3367:       return {
 3368:         draw: item.draw,
 3369:         scene: item.scene,
 3370:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
 3371:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
 3372:         score,
 3373:       };
 3374:     })
 3375:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
 3376:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3377:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
 3378:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3379:       }
 3380: 
 3381:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3382:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3383:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
 3384:     })
 3385:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
 3386: }
 3387: 
 3388: 
 3389: 
 3390: 
 3391: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 3392:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
 3393:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 3394: 
 3395:   const freq = new Map();
 3396:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 3397: 
 3398:   let totalWeight = 0;
 3399: 
 3400:   for (const item of ranking) {
 3401:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
 3402:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
 3403: 
 3404:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
 3405:     if (score <= 0) continue;
 3406: 
 3407:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
 3408:     totalWeight += score;
 3409:   }
 3410: 
 3411:   const prob = new Map();
 3412:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 3413:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
 3414:   }
 3415: 
 3416:   return {
 3417:     freq,
 3418:     prob,
 3419:     samples: ranking.length,
 3420:     totalWeight,
 3421:   };
 3422: }
 3423: 
 3424: 
 3425: /* =========================
 3426:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
 3427: ========================= */
 3428: 
 3429: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
 3430:   const g = Number(grupo2);
 3431:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
 3432: 
 3433:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
 3434:   const out = [];
 3435: 
 3436:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
 3437:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
 3438:   }
 3439: 
 3440:   return out.filter(Boolean);
 3441: }
 3442: 
 3443: 
 3444: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
 3445:   rangeDraws,
 3446:   analysisHourBucket,
 3447:   schedule,
 3448:   grupo2,
 3449:   count = 20,
 3450:   targetYmd = "",
 3451: }) {
 3452:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
 3453:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
 3454:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
 3455:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
 3456:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
 3457: 
 3458:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
 3459:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
 3460:     : 20;
 3461: 
 3462:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
 3463: 
 3464:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
 3465:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
 3466:   }
 3467: 
 3468:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
 3469:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
 3470: 
 3471:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
 3472:   const byMilhar = new Map();
 3473: 
 3474:   for (const d of list) {
 3475:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3476:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3477:     if (!h) continue;
 3478: 
 3479:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
 3480:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
 3481:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
 3482:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3483: 
 3484:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
 3485: 
 3486:     for (const prize of ps) {
 3487:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
 3488:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
 3489: 
 3490:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
 3491:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
 3492: 
 3493:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3494:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
 3495: 
 3496:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
 3497:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
 3498: 
 3499:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
 3500: 
 3501:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
 3502:         milhar: m4,
 3503:         dezena: dz,
 3504:         centena: c3,
 3505:         freq: 0,
 3506:         score: 0,
 3507:         targetHits: 0,
 3508:         scheduleHits: 0,
 3509:         sameDowHits: 0,
 3510:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
 3511:         lastTs: 0,
 3512:       };
 3513: 
 3514:       let inc = 1;
 3515: 
 3516:       if (isTargetHour) {
 3517:         inc += 8;
 3518:         current.targetHits += 1;
 3519:       }
 3520: 
 3521:       if (isScheduleHour) {
 3522:         inc += 3;
 3523:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
 3524:       }
 3525: 
 3526:       if (isSameDow) {
 3527:         inc += 2;
 3528:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
 3529:       }
 3530: 
 3531:       if (pos === 1) {
 3532:         inc += 3;
 3533:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
 3534:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
 3535:         inc += 1;
 3536:       }
 3537: 
 3538:       current.freq += 1;
 3539:       current.score += inc;
 3540: 
 3541:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
 3542:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
 3543:       }
 3544: 
 3545:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
 3546:     }
 3547:   }
 3548: 
 3549:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
 3550:     .map((m) => {
 3551:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
 3552:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
 3553:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
 3554:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
 3555:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
 3556:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
 3557: 
 3558:       return {
 3559:         ...m,
 3560:         score:
 3561:           freq * 100 +
 3562:           targetHits * 70 +
 3563:           scheduleHits * 35 +
 3564:           sameDowHits * 25 +
 3565:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
 3566:           recencyBoost * 5,
 3567:       };
 3568:     })
 3569:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3570:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3571:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
 3572:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
 3573:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
 3574:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
 3575:     });
 3576: 
 3577:   const centenaStats = new Map();
 3578: 
 3579:   for (const item of ranked) {
 3580:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
 3581:     if (!centena) continue;
 3582: 
 3583:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
 3584:       centena,
 3585:       freq: 0,
 3586:       score: 0,
 3587:       targetHits: 0,
 3588:       sameDowHits: 0,
 3589:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
 3590:       lastTs: 0,
 3591:     };
 3592: 
 3593:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
 3594:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
 3595:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
 3596:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
 3597:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
 3598:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
 3599: 
 3600:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
 3601:   }
 3602: 
 3603:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
 3604: 
 3605:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
 3606:     centenaScoreMap.set(
 3607:       c.centena,
 3608:       Number(c.score || 0) +
 3609:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
 3610:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
 3611:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
 3612:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
 3613:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
 3614:     );
 3615:   }
 3616: 
 3617:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
 3618:   const slots = [];
 3619: 
 3620:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
 3621:     const candidates = ranked
 3622:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
 3623:       .map((x) => {
 3624:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3625:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
 3626:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
 3627:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
 3628: 
 3629:         return {
 3630:           ...x,
 3631:           milhar,
 3632:           prefix,
 3633:           centena,
 3634:           centenaScore,
 3635:           adjustedScore:
 3636:             centenaScore * 2 +
 3637:             Number(x.score || 0) -
 3638:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
 3639:         };
 3640:       });
 3641: 
 3642:     const byCentena = new Map();
 3643: 
 3644:     for (const item of candidates) {
 3645:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
 3646:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
 3647:     }
 3648: 
 3649:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
 3650:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
 3651:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
 3652:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
 3653:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
 3654:           }
 3655:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3656:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
 3657:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
 3658:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
 3659:         });
 3660: 
 3661:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
 3662: 
 3663:         return {
 3664:           centena,
 3665:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
 3666:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
 3667:           items: sortedItems,
 3668:         };
 3669:       })
 3670:       .sort((a, b) => {
 3671:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
 3672:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
 3673:         }
 3674:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
 3675:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
 3676:         }
 3677:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
 3678:       });
 3679: 
 3680:     const picked = [];
 3681:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
 3682:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
 3683: 
 3684:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
 3685:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3686:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3687: 
 3688:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
 3689:       if (!item) continue;
 3690: 
 3691:       picked.push(item);
 3692:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
 3693:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
 3694:     }
 3695: 
 3696:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
 3697:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3698:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3699:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
 3700: 
 3701:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
 3702:       if (!item) continue;
 3703: 
 3704:       picked.push(item);
 3705:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
 3706:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
 3707:     }
 3708: 
 3709:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
 3710:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3711:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3712: 
 3713:       for (const item of group.items) {
 3714:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3715:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
 3716: 
 3717:         picked.push(item);
 3718:       }
 3719:     }
 3720: 
 3721:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
 3722:   }
 3723: 
 3724:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
 3725:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
 3726: 
 3727:     let pushed = 0;
 3728: 
 3729:     for (const item of items) {
 3730:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
 3731:       slots.push({
 3732:         dezena: dz,
 3733:         milhar: item.milhar,
 3734:         score: item.score,
 3735:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
 3736:         centena: item.centena,
 3737:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
 3738:         freq: item.freq,
 3739:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
 3740:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
 3741:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
 3742:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
 3743:       });
 3744:       pushed += 1;
 3745:     }
 3746: 
 3747:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
 3748:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
 3749:       pushed += 1;
 3750:     }
 3751:   }
 3752: 
 3753:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
 3754: 
 3755:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
 3756: }
 3757: 
 3758: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
 3759:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
 3760: }
 3761: 
 3762: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
 3763:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
 3764: }
 3765: 
 3766: /* =========================
 3767:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
 3768: ========================= */
 3769: 
 3770: 
 3771: 
 3772: export function auditTop3Timeline({
 3773:   timeline,
 3774:   lotteryKey = "",
 3775: }) {
 3776:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
 3777: 
 3778:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
 3779:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
 3780:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
 3781:   });
 3782: 
 3783:   const total = validated.length;
 3784: 
 3785:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
 3786:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
 3787:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
 3788:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
 3789:   }).length;
 3790: 
 3791:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
 3792:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
 3793:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3794:       .slice(0, 3)
 3795:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
 3796:   }).length;
 3797: 
 3798:   function pct(n, d) {
 3799:     if (!d) return 0;
 3800:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
 3801:   }
 3802: 
 3803:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
 3804:     const map = new Map();
 3805: 
 3806:     for (const slot of validated) {
 3807:       const key = keyFn(slot);
 3808:       const current = map.get(key) || {
 3809:         key,
 3810:         total: 0,
 3811:         top1Hits: 0,
 3812:         top3Hits: 0,
 3813:       };
 3814: 
 3815:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
 3816:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
 3817:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
 3818: 
 3819:       current.total += 1;
 3820:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
 3821:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
 3822: 
 3823:       map.set(key, current);
 3824:     }
 3825: 
 3826:     return Array.from(map.values())
 3827:       .map((x) => ({
 3828:         ...x,
 3829:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
 3830:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
 3831:       }))
 3832:       .sort((a, b) => {
 3833:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
 3834:         return b.total - a.total;
 3835:       });
 3836:   }
 3837: 
 3838:   return {
 3839:     lotteryKey,
 3840:     total,
 3841:     top1Hits,
 3842:     top3Hits,
 3843:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
 3844:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
 3845:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
 3846:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
 3847:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
 3848:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
 3849:       hour: slot.targetHour,
 3850:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
 3851:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
 3852:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
 3853:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3854:         .slice(0, 3)
 3855:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
 3856:       top1Hit:
 3857:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
 3858:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3859:         .slice(0, 3)
 3860:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
 3861:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
 3862:     })),
 3863:   };
 3864: }
 3865: 
 3866: 
 3867: function buildTimelineForDate({
 3868:   ymd,
 3869:   drawsToday,
 3870:   drawsRange,
 3871:   lotteryKey,
 3872:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 3873:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 3874:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 3875: }) {
 3876:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
 3877:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
 3878:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
 3879: 
 3880:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
 3881: 
 3882:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
 3883:     lotteryKey,
 3884:     ymd: targetYmd,
 3885:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 3886:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 3887:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 3888:   });
 3889: 
 3890:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
 3891:     .map(toHourBucket)
 3892:     .filter(Boolean);
 3893: 
 3894:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
 3895: 
 3896:   const daySorted = day
 3897:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
 3898:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3899:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 3900:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 3901:       return ta - tb;
 3902:     });
 3903: 
 3904:   const rangeSorted = range
 3905:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
 3906:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3907:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 3908:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 3909:       return ta - tb;
 3910:     });
 3911: 
 3912:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
 3913: 
 3914:   for (const d of daySorted) {
 3915:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3916:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3917:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
 3918:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
 3919:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
 3920:   }
 3921: 
 3922: 
 3923:   const chainContextDraws = [];
 3924:   const timeline = [];
 3925:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
 3926: 
 3927:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
 3928:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
 3929:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
 3930: 
 3931: 
 3932:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
 3933:       timeline.push({
 3934:         targetYmd,
 3935:         targetHour: slotHour,
 3936:         baseYmd: "",
 3937:         baseHour: "",
 3938:         top3: [],
 3939:         resultGrupo: null,
 3940:         hit: null,
 3941:         status: "pending",
 3942:       });
 3943:       continue;
 3944:     }
 3945: 
 3946:     let baseDraw = null;
 3947: 
 3948:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
 3949:       .filter((d) => {
 3950:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3951:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3952:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3953:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
 3954:       })
 3955:       .sort((a, b) => {
 3956:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 3957:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 3958:         return tb - ta;
 3959:       })[0] || null;
 3960: 
 3961:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
 3962: 
 3963:     if (!baseDraw) {
 3964:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
 3965:         .filter((d) => {
 3966:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3967:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3968:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3969:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
 3970:         })
 3971:         .sort((a, b) => {
 3972:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 3973:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 3974:           return tb - ta;
 3975:         })[0] || null;
 3976: 
 3977:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
 3978:     }
 3979: 
 3980:     if (!baseDraw) {
 3981:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
 3982:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
 3983:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3984:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3985:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3986: 
 3987:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
 3988: 
 3989:         baseDraw = d;
 3990:         break;
 3991:       }
 3992:     }
 3993: 
 3994:     if (!baseDraw) {
 3995:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
 3996:     }
 3997: 
 3998:     if (!baseDraw) {
 3999:       timeline.push({
 4000:         targetYmd,
 4001:         targetHour: slotHour,
 4002:         baseYmd: "",
 4003:         baseHour: "",
 4004:         top3: [],
 4005:         resultGrupo: null,
 4006:         hit: null,
 4007:         status: "pending",
 4008:       });
 4009:       continue;
 4010:     }
 4011: 
 4012:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
 4013:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
 4014:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
 4015:     );
 4016: 
 4017:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
 4018:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4019:         pickDrawYMD(d),
 4020:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4021:       );
 4022:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
 4023:     });
 4024: 
 4025:     const historyStats = (() => {
 4026:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
 4027: 
 4028:       let d30 = 0;
 4029:       let d90 = 0;
 4030:       let d180 = 0;
 4031:       let d365 = 0;
 4032: 
 4033:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
 4034:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
 4035:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
 4036:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
 4037:         );
 4038: 
 4039:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
 4040: 
 4041:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
 4042: 
 4043:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
 4044:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
 4045:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
 4046:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
 4047:       }
 4048: 
 4049:       return {
 4050:         total: usableHistory.length,
 4051:         d30,
 4052:         d90,
 4053:         d180,
 4054:         d365,
 4055:       };
 4056:     })();
 4057: 
 4058:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
 4059:       .filter((d) => {
 4060:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4061:           pickDrawYMD(d),
 4062:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4063:         );
 4064:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
 4065:       })
 4066:       .sort((a, b) => {
 4067:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4068:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4069:         return ta - tb;
 4070:       });
 4071: 
 4072:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
 4073:       lotteryKey,
 4074:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
 4075:       drawLast: baseDraw,
 4076:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
 4077:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4078:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4079:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4080:       topN: 3,
 4081:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
 4082:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
 4083:     });
 4084: 
 4085: 
 4086:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
 4087:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
 4088:     const computedForSlot =
 4089:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
 4090: 
 4091:     const top3 =
 4092:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
 4093:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
 4094:         : [];
 4095: 
 4096:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
 4097: 
 4098:     const hit =
 4099:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
 4100:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
 4101:         : null;
 4102: 
 4103:     timeline.push({
 4104:       targetYmd,
 4105:       targetHour: slotHour,
 4106:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
 4107:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
 4108:       top3,
 4109:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
 4110:       hit,
 4111:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
 4112:       historyStats,
 4113:     });
 4114: 
 4115:     if (currentDraw) {
 4116:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
 4117:     } else {
 4118:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
 4119:     }
 4120:   }
 4121: 
 4122:   return timeline;
 4123: 
 4124: }
 4125: 
 4126: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
 4127:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
 4128: }
 4129: 
 4130: export function auditTop3Backtest({
 4131:   drawsRange,
 4132:   lotteryKey,
 4133:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4134:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4135:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4136: }) {
 4137:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
 4138:   const dates = Array.from(
 4139:     new Set(
 4140:       range
 4141:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
 4142:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
 4143:     )
 4144:   ).sort();
 4145: 
 4146:   const allTimeline = [];
 4147: 
 4148:   for (const ymd of dates) {
 4149:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
 4150: 
 4151:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
 4152:       ymd,
 4153:       drawsToday,
 4154:       drawsRange: range,
 4155:       lotteryKey,
 4156:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4157:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4158:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4159:     });
 4160: 
 4161:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
 4162:   }
 4163: 
 4164:   return auditTop3Timeline({
 4165:     timeline: allTimeline,
 4166:     lotteryKey,
 4167:   });
 4168: }

========================================================================================================================
src/pages/Top3/modules/scoreEngine/scoreEngineV2.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: import scoreConfig from "./scoreConfig";
    4: import { collectEvidence } from "./evidenceEngine";
    5: 
    6: /**
    7:  * Score Engine V2 inicial
    8:  *
    9:  * Consome evidências.
   10:  * Não aplica regra cega isolada.
   11:  */
   12: 
   13: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
   14:   const n = Number(value);
   15:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
   16:   return n;
   17: }
   18: 
   19: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
   20:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
   21: 
   22:   if (!list.length) {
   23:     return {
   24:       score: 0,
   25:       confidence: 0,
   26:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
   27:       signals: {},
   28:     };
   29:   }
   30: 
   31:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
   32:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
   33:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
   34: 
   35:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
   36:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
   37: 
   38:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
   39: 
   40:   const signals = {};
   41:   for (const e of list) {
   42:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   return {
   46:     score,
   47:     confidence,
   48:     reasons,
   49:     signals,
   50:   };
   51: }
   52: 
   53: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
   54:   const collected = collectEvidence({
   55:     item,
   56:     context,
   57:     config: scoreConfig,
   58:   });
   59: 
   60:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
   61: 
   62:   const probability = Math.max(
   63:     0,
   64:     Math.min(
   65:       100,
   66:       Number(item.scoreProb || item.rawScoreProb || 0) * 100
   67:     )
   68:   );
   69: 
   70:   const finalScore = Math.round(
   71:     (strength.score * 0.45) +
   72:     (probability * 0.55)
   73:   );
   74: 
   75:   const finalConfidence = Math.round(
   76:     (strength.confidence * 0.40) +
   77:     (probability * 0.60)
   78:   );
   79: 
   80:   return {
   81:     ...item,
   82: 
   83:     score: finalScore,
   84: 
   85:     confidence: finalConfidence,
   86: 
   87:     reasons: [
   88:       ...strength.reasons,
   89:       `Probabilidade V3: ${probability.toFixed(2)}%`,
   90:     ],
   91: 
   92:     signals: {
   93:       ...strength.signals,
   94:       probability,
   95:     },
   96: 
   97:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
   98: 
   99:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
  100:   };
  101: }
  102: 
  103: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
  104: 
  105:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
  106:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
  107:     .sort((a, b) => {
  108: 
  109:       if (b.score !== a.score)
  110:         return b.score - a.score;
  111: 
  112:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
  113:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
  114: 
  115:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
  116: 
  117:     });
  118: 
  119: }
  120: 
  121: export {
  122: scoreItem,
  123:   scoreRanking,
  124:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
  125: };

src/pages/Top3/modules/scoreEngine/evidenceEngine.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: import { buildFrequencyEvidence } from "./frequencyEvidence";
    4: import { buildContextEvidence } from "./contextEvidence";
    5: 
    6: /**
    7:  * Evidence Engine
    8:  *
    9:  * Centraliza todos os módulos de evidência.
   10:  * Cada módulo produz fatos; nenhum decide o resultado.
   11:  */
   12: 
   13: const MODULES = {
   14:   frequency: buildFrequencyEvidence,
   15:   context: buildContextEvidence,
   16: };
   17: 
   18: function collectEvidence({
   19:   item = {},
   20:   context = {},
   21:   config = {},
   22: } = {}) {
   23: 
   24:   const evidence = [];
   25:   const enabled = config.evidenceModules || {};
   26: 
   27:   for (const [name, builder] of Object.entries(MODULES)) {
   28: 
   29:     if (!enabled[name]?.enabled) {
   30:       continue;
   31:     }
   32: 
   33:     try {
   34: 
   35:       const result = builder(item, context);
   36: 
   37:       if (result) {
   38:         evidence.push(result);
   39:       }
   40: 
   41:     } catch (err) {
   42: 
   43:       evidence.push({
   44:         module: name,
   45:         error: true,
   46:         message: err.message,
   47:       });
   48: 
   49:     }
   50: 
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   return {
   54: 
   55:     count: evidence.filter(e => !e.error).length,
   56: 
   57:     modules: evidence
   58:       .filter(e => !e.error)
   59:       .map(e => e.module),
   60: 
   61:     evidence,
   62: 
   63:   };
   64: 
   65: }
   66: 
   67: export {
   68: collectEvidence,
   69: };

src/pages/Top3/modules/scoreEngine/contextEvidence.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: /**
    4:  * Context Evidence V2
    5:  *
    6:  * Responsabilidade:
    7:  * Produzir evidências do contexto do sorteio.
    8:  * Não calcula score.
    9:  */
   10: 
   11: function normalize(value) {
   12:     const n = Number(value);
   13:     return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: function buildReasons(ctx) {
   17: 
   18:     const reasons = [];
   19: 
   20:     reasons.push("Contexto histórico identificado.");
   21: 
   22:     if (ctx.lotteryKey)
   23:         reasons.push(`Loteria: ${ctx.lotteryKey}`);
   24: 
   25:     if (ctx.weekday)
   26:         reasons.push(`Dia da semana: ${ctx.weekday}`);
   27: 
   28:     if (ctx.closeHour)
   29:         reasons.push(`Horário: ${ctx.closeHour}`);
   30: 
   31:     if (ctx.previousGroup != null)
   32:         reasons.push(`Grupo anterior: ${ctx.previousGroup}`);
   33: 
   34:     if (ctx.previousAnimal)
   35:         reasons.push(`Animal anterior: ${ctx.previousAnimal}`);
   36: 
   37:     return reasons;
   38: }
   39: 
   40: function buildContextEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   41: 
   42:     const evidence = {
   43: 
   44:         lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
   45: 
   46:         ymd: context.ymd || null,
   47: 
   48:         weekday: context.weekday || null,
   49: 
   50:         closeHour: context.hour || null,
   51: 
   52:         prize: normalize(context.prize),
   53: 
   54:         previousAnimal: context.previousAnimal || null,
   55: 
   56:         previousGroup: normalize(context.previousGroup),
   57: 
   58:         previousHour: context.previousHour || null,
   59: 
   60:         lookback: normalize(context.lookback),
   61: 
   62:         window: normalize(context.window),
   63: 
   64:     };
   65: 
   66:     return {
   67: 
   68:         module: "context",
   69: 
   70:         value: 1,
   71: 
   72:         evidence,
   73: 
   74:         reasons: buildReasons(evidence),
   75: 
   76:     };
   77: 
   78: }
   79: 
   80: export {
   81: buildContextEvidence,
   82: };

========================================================================================================================
src/pages/Top3/modules/scoreEngine/frequencyEvidence.js
========================================================================================================================
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: /**
    4:  * Frequency Evidence V2
    5:  *
    6:  * Responsabilidade:
    7:  * Produzir evidências relacionadas à frequência.
    8:  * Não calcula score.
    9:  */
   10: 
   11: function normalize(value) {
   12:   const n = Number(value);
   13: 
   14:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
   15:     return 0;
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   return n;
   19: }
   20: 
   21: function normalizePercent(value) {
   22:   const n = Number(value);
   23: 
   24:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
   25:     return 0;
   26:   }
   27: 
   28:   return Math.min(100, n);
   29: }
   30: 
   31: function classifyFrequency(percent) {
   32:   if (percent >= 70) return "very_high";
   33:   if (percent >= 50) return "high";
   34:   if (percent >= 30) return "medium";
   35:   if (percent >= 15) return "low";
   36:   return "very_low";
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function buildReasons(frequency, percent, level) {
   40:   const reasons = [];
   41: 
   42:   reasons.push(`Frequência histórica: ${frequency}`);
   43: 
   44:   if (percent > 0) {
   45:     reasons.push(`Participação: ${percent.toFixed(1)}%`);
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   switch (level) {
   49:     case "very_high":
   50:       reasons.push("Frequência muito elevada.");
   51:       break;
   52: 
   53:     case "high":
   54:       reasons.push("Frequência elevada.");
   55:       break;
   56: 
   57:     case "medium":
   58:       reasons.push("Frequência intermediária.");
   59:       break;
   60: 
   61:     case "low":
   62:       reasons.push("Frequência baixa.");
   63:       break;
   64: 
   65:     default:
   66:       reasons.push("Pouca evidência de frequência.");
   67:   }
   68: 
   69:   return reasons;
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function buildFrequencyEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   73:   const frequency = normalize(
   74:     item.frequency ??
   75:     item.total ??
   76:     item.count ??
   77:     item.appearances
   78:   );
   79: 
   80:   const totalUniverse = normalize(
   81:     context.totalDraws ??
   82:     context.windowSize ??
   83:     context.sampleSize
   84:   );
   85: 
   86:   const percent =
   87:     totalUniverse > 0
   88:       ? normalizePercent((frequency / totalUniverse) * 100)
   89:       : normalizePercent(
   90:           item.frequencyPercent ??
   91:           item.frequencyRate
   92:         );
   93: 
   94:   const level = classifyFrequency(percent);
   95: 
   96:   return {
   97:     module: "frequency",
   98: 
   99:     value: percent,
  100: 
  101:     evidence: {
  102:       frequency,
  103:       percent,
  104:       level,
  105:       totalUniverse,
  106: 
  107:       window: context.window || null,
  108:       lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
  109:       position: context.position || null,
  110:       weekday: context.weekday || null,
  111:       hour: context.hour || null,
  112:     },
  113: 
  114:     reasons: buildReasons(frequency, percent, level),
  115:   };
  116: }
  117: 
  118: export {
  119: buildFrequencyEvidence,
  120: };
